## DLC_TOXOIDS_RECOMMENDATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_TOXOIDS_RECOMMENDATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在§SToxoids§!中，您将与群星历史上最具韧性的物种并肩同行！开发有毒星球、为眼前利益押注星球的未来，在这充满敌意的寰宇中，做出艰难却必要的抉择，以求生存与发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:3`

### English

In §SToxoids§!, stake your claim with the hardiest, most stubborn species in Stellaris' history! Exploit toxic worlds, gamble the future of your planets for immediate gains, and make the tough sacrifices necessary to survive a hostile galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:4`

## origin_toxic_knights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_toxic_knights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几个世纪前，这个物种的母星被一位毒性神祗造访。接踵而至的生态灾难几乎摧毁了这个星球，但是幸存者们怀着对毒圣的崇敬之情，草草地统一了起来。他们创立了一个骑士教团来搜寻毒圣，尽管后来很多事情变了，但是这份试炼依旧保留了下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:11`

### English

Centuries ago, this species' homeworld was visited by a Toxic Entity. The ecological catastrophe that followed ruined half the world, but allowed an early unification of the survivors in their reverence for the Toxic God. An Order of Knights has been created to search for the Toxic God, and although many things have changed, the quest continues.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:14`

## START_SCREEN_TOXIC_KNIGHTS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_TOXIC_KNIGHTS&lang=en)

### 中文

很久以前，我们的行星四分五裂。沐猴而冠的所谓国王们互相征伐，无数的人口为之白白牺牲。但第一位至高王的到来改变了一切。天赐与他的领导能力铸就了一个无与伦比的王国。\n\n至高王虔诚无比。他向一切一切不信者宣战，但异端仍野火烧不尽。直到一天，毒圣降临到了我们的星球，至高王的祈祷得到了回应。尽管祂的到来只是短短一瞬，但祂剧毒的影响力瞬间毒化了所有不信者之地。所有幸存者纷纷效忠至高王。我们的星球第一次获得了统一。\n\n毒圣很快又离我们而去，但至高王却决定找到祂，为此建立了一个骑士团。数个世纪以来，这些英勇的灵魂们孜孜不倦地寻找，但一无所获。毒圣已经离开我们的星球太久了。试炼的下一个章节将会在星际间谱写。利用强大的遗珍，骑士团建立了我们帝国第一个空间站。坚固无比的主城将会是他们飞向茫茫星海的跳板。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:22`

### English

Long ago, our planet was divided. Petty kings squabbled constantly, and our population bled for their vain ambitions. But with the coming of the first High King, all of this changed. Their inspired leadership forged a kingdom without equal.\n\nAbove all else, the High King was pious. War was waged against any who denied their God, but still the heretics persisted. The King's prayers were answered when the Toxic God visited our world. Though its time among us was brief, its baneful influence poisoned the lands of the unbelievers. Any who survived pledged fealty to the High King. For the first time, our world was unified.\n\nThe Toxic God soon left us, but the King, determined to find it, founded an order of knights. For centuries, these valiant souls searched tirelessly, but to no avail. The Toxic God had long since departed from our world. The next chapter of the Quest would be written in the stars. With the use of powerful relics, it was the Order that built our Empire's first orbital station. A mighty Keep, it would become the springboard from which they plunged ever deeper into the cosmos.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:26`

## START_SCREEN_TOXIC_KNIGHTS_CORPORATE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_TOXIC_KNIGHTS_CORPORATE&lang=en)

### 中文

很久以前，我们的行星四分五裂。沐猴而冠的所谓国王们互相征伐，无数的人口为之白白牺牲。但第一位至高王的到来改变了一切。天赐与他的领导能力铸就了一个无与伦比的王国。\n\n至高王虔诚无比。他向一切一切不信者宣战，但异端仍野火烧不尽。直到一天，毒圣降临到了我们的星球，至高王的祈祷得到了回应。尽管祂的到来只是短短一瞬，但祂剧毒的影响力瞬间毒化了所有不信者之地。所有幸存者纷纷效忠至高王。我们的星球第一次获得了统一。\n\n毒圣很快又离我们而去，但至高王却决定找到祂，为此建立了一个骑士团。数个世纪以来，这些英勇的灵魂们孜孜不倦地寻找，但一无所获。试炼是一个耗费高昂的事业。随着时间的推移，这个王国越来越感兴趣于追名逐利以养活骑士们。无论是生活还是消费，每一位公民都是在支持试炼。\n\n毒圣已经离开我们的星球太久了。试炼的下一个章节将会在星际间谱写。利用强大的遗珍，骑士团建立了我们帝国第一个空间站。坚固无比的主城将会是他们飞向茫茫星海的跳板。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

Long ago, our planet was divided. Petty kings squabbled constantly, and our population bled for their vain ambitions. But with the coming of the first High King, all of this changed. Their inspired leadership forged a kingdom without equal.\n\nAbove all else, the High King was pious. War was waged against any who denied their God, but still the heretics persisted. The King's prayers were answered when the Toxic God visited our world. Though its time among us was brief, its baneful influence poisoned the lands of the unbelievers. Any who survived pledged fealty to the High King. For the first time, our world was unified.\n\nThe Toxic God soon left us, but the King, determined to find it, founded an order of knights. For centuries, these valiant souls searched tirelessly, but to no avail. The Quest is a very expensive prospect. Over time, the kingdom became more and more interested in the pursuit of profit to better support the knights. By living and consuming, every citizen supports the Quest, and our champions are heavily mediatized.\n\nThe Toxic God has long since departed from our world and the next chapter of the Quest would be written in the stars. With the use of powerful relics, it was the Order that built our Empire's first orbital station. A mighty Keep, it would become the springboard from which they plunged ever deeper into the cosmos.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:27`

## START_SCREEN_TOXIC_KNIGHTS_NOMADS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_TOXIC_KNIGHTS_NOMADS&lang=en)

### 中文

很久以前，我们的行星四分五裂。沐猴而冠的所谓国王们互相征伐，无数的人口为之白白牺牲。但第一位至高王的到来改变了一切。天赐与他的领导能力铸就了一个无与伦比的王国。\n\n至高王虔诚无比。他向一切一切不信者宣战，但异端仍野火烧不尽。直到一天，毒圣降临到了我们的星球，至高王的祈祷得到了回应。尽管祂的到来只是短短一瞬，但祂剧毒的影响力瞬间毒化了所有不信者之地。所有幸存者纷纷效忠至高王。我们的星球第一次获得了统一。\n\n尽管毒圣已经离去，但祂的影响仍在扩散，很快让整个星球不再宜居。至高王却决定拯救我们，为此建立了一个骑士团。数个世纪以来，这些英勇的灵魂们孜孜不倦地寻找，但一无所获。毒圣已经离开我们的星球太久了。试炼的下一个章节将会在星际间谱写。利用强大的遗珍，骑士团建造了我们帝国第一艘方舟舰。当我们启程踏上穿越星辰的征途时，这艘移动的主城将庇护我们最后的人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:24`

### English

Long ago, our planet was divided. Petty kings squabbled constantly, and our population bled for their vain ambitions. But with the coming of the first High King, all of this changed. Their inspired leadership forged a kingdom without equal.\n\nAbove all else, the High King was pious. War was waged against any who denied their God, but still the heretics persisted. The King's prayers were answered when the Toxic God visited our world. Though its time among us was brief, its baneful influence poisoned the lands of the unbelievers. Any who survived pledged fealty to the High King. For the first time, our world was unified.\n\nThough Toxic God left us, its influence kept spreading, slowly rendering the planet uninhabitable. The King, determined to save us, founded an order of knights. For centuries, these valiant souls searched tirelessly, but to no avail. The Toxic God had long since departed from our world. The next chapter of the Quest would be written in the stars. With the use of powerful relics, it was the Order that built our Empire's first arkship. A mobile Keep, it would shelter the last of our population as we embarked at one to take our quest across the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:28`

## job_knight_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_knight_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为战而训，品学兼优。这些骑士们代表着我们社会的精粹，他们全身心地奉献一切，只为寻求毒圣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

Trained for combat; well educated; virtuous. These knights represent the very best of our society, and they have dedicated their lives to the search for the Toxic God.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:31`

## job_knight_effect_desc

## job_squire_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_squire_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

成为一名骑士需要数年的训练。那些谋求进入骑士团的人必须从侍奉一名骑士做起，以他们为楷模学习，甚至要努力让他们的贵族生活过的舒适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:37`

### English

Becoming a knight takes years of training. Those seeking admission into the Order must first serve a knight, learning from their example even as they strive to make their noble lives more comfortable.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:42`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:38`

### English

## job_knight_commander_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_knight_commander_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士们富有荣誉，值得尊敬——是我们社会能够培养出的最为出类拔萃的一批人。但即使是他们也需要有人领导。骑士领主剑锋所指，骑士同胞旌旗必达！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

The knights are worthy and honorable - the best our society has to offer. But even they need leadership. Where the Lord Commander leads, there shall the knights follow.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:53`

## job_knight_commander_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_knight_commander_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£job_knight_commander£ $job_knight_commander_plural$将$r_alloys$转化为$r_unity$和$r_amenities$，并提高£mod_country_naval_cap_add£ §Y$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§!。\n完成§Y骑士誓言试炼§!将进一步提升其产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

£job_knight_commander£ $job_knight_commander_plural$ turn $r_alloys$ into $r_unity$ and $r_amenities$, and increase £mod_country_naval_cap_add£ §Y$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§!.\nCompleting §YKnightly Quests§! can further improve their output.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:54`

## job_knight_commander_research_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_knight_commander_research_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

£job_knight_commander£ $job_knight_commander_plural$将$r_alloys$转化为$r_unity$、$r_research$和$r_amenities$并提高£mod_country_naval_cap_add£ §Y$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§!。\n完成§Y骑士誓言试炼§!将进一步提升其产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

£job_knight_commander£ $job_knight_commander_plural$ turn $r_alloys$ into $r_unity$, $r_research$, and $r_amenities$, and increase £mod_country_naval_cap_add£ §Y$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§!.\nCompleting §YKnightly Quests§! can further improve their output.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:55`

## district_orders_demesne_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistrict_orders_demesne_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团的势力不应该局限于任何要塞或恒星基地！不，只有广袤无垠的开放空间——满是资源和优质猎场的星球和恒星系——才能让他们真正的繁荣壮大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

The Order shall not be limited to any keep or star base! Nay, only with vast open spaces - worlds and systems bountiful with resources and good hunting - can they truly thrive.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:63`

## building_order_keep_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_order_keep_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团的宅邸是骑士成员的归家。它收录了海量的数据，装满了其漫长而闪耀的历史长河中缴获的各类战利品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:63`

### English

The seat of the Order is the home of its knights. It boasts vast archives of data, and is filled with trophies collected over its long and illustrious history.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:73`

## d_toxic_god_pools_most_venemous_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_pools_most_venemous_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神明身后留下了许多最毒之池，在此可以寻觅到残留的毒物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

Left in the Deity's wake are many pools most venemous, from which remnants of Its toxins may be recovered.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:81`

## d_toxic_god_pools_most_venemous_upgraded

### English

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:82`

## d_toxic_god_pools_most_venemous_upgraded_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_pools_most_venemous_upgraded_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

神明身后留下了许多最毒之池，在此可以寻觅到残留的毒物。幸亏有了毒圣骑士的辛苦耕耘，我们现在能够收集这些强有力的毒药，并开发出多种多样的用途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

Left in the Deity's wake are many pools most venomous, from which remnants of Its toxins may be recovered. Thanks to the efforts of the Knights of the Toxic God, we are now able to harvest and make diverse use of this potent poison.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:83`

## d_toxic_god_pestilential_wasteland_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_pestilential_wasteland_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

并非每个人都能享受神明的赐福。这片土地的大部分都在神明经过之时被摧毁，永远无法恢复，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Not all could enjoy the blessings of the Deity. Large areas of land were ruined by its passing, never to recover.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:85`

### 中文

$d_toxic_god_pestilential_wasteland_desc$然而，经骑士之手复原的装甲套装让我们得以在这些区域安全地工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

$d_toxic_god_pestilential_wasteland_desc$ However, the suits of armor recovered by the knights have provided us with the means to work safely in these parts.

## d_toxic_god_deitys_swarms

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_deitys_swarms&lang=en)

### 中文

圣临虫群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

Swarms of the Deity

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:88`

## d_toxic_god_deitys_swarms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_deitys_swarms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在神明身后，之前在这个星球从未见过的昆虫迅速蔓延开来，并在众多受影响的区域繁衍壮大。尽管我们试图耕耘这些土地，这些害虫的出现依然持续地对我们造成了挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

In the Deity's wake, insects previously unknown to this world spread rapidly, coming to thrive in many of the affected areas. Their pestilent presence continues to test us as we seek to cultivate these lands.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:89`

## d_toxic_god_deitys_swarms_upgraded

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_deitys_swarms_upgraded&lang=en)

### 中文

驯化圣虫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Subdued Swarms

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:90`

## d_toxic_god_deitys_swarms_upgraded_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_deitys_swarms_upgraded_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在神明身后，之前在这个星球从未见过的昆虫迅速蔓延开来，并在众多受影响的区域繁衍壮大。然而，因为它们多汁的肉质和强力的毒药，我们已经充分掌握了养殖它们的技艺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

In the Deity's wake, insects previously unknown to this world spread rapidly, coming to thrive in many of the affected areas. We have mastered the art of farming them for their succulent taste and potent venom.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:91`

## d_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land&lang=en)

### 中文

枯潮大地

### English

A Blight Upon the Land

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:92`

## d_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据说这里是神明第一次因为愤怒毁灭大地。过去的事物几乎没有什么能够幸存下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

It is said that in this place, the Deity first smote the land in anger. Little remains of what once was here.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:93`

## d_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land_upgraded

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land_upgraded&lang=en)

### 中文

枯潮之忆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

Blighted Remembrance

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:94`

## d_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land_upgraded_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_toxic_god_blight_upon_the_land_upgraded_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

据说这里是神明第一次因为愤怒毁灭大地。过去的事物几乎没有什么能够幸存下来。然而，一座纪念碑被竖立了起来，它象征着我们彼时的回忆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

It is said that in this place, the Deity first smote the land in anger. Little remains of what once was here, but a monument has been erected, its features synthesized from our memories of that moment.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:95`

## syamelles_curse_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asyamelles_curse_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个神秘存在诅咒我们永远得不到真正的爱与欲望。同时她也给予了我们更加机械化的繁衍方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

A mysterious being cursed us to never again know true love and desire. At the same time, however, she granted us to procreate by more mechanical means.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:135`

## syamelles_curse_habitat

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asyamelles_curse_habitat&lang=en)

### 中文

情人的疱疹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Lover's Pox

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:136`

## syamelles_curse_habitat_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asyamelles_curse_habitat_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒的诅咒的效果在骑士团的总部感受最为强烈。人们开始对身体接触感到极不适应，于是扈从们不得不寻找新的方法来补充人口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

The effects of Syamelle's Curse are felt hardest at the Order's Headquarters. So unnatural has the idea of physical contact become that the Squires have been forced to find new ways to replenish the population.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:137`

## syamelles_curse_habitat_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asyamelles_curse_habitat_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

$MOD_POP_GROWTH_SPEED_REDUCTION$：§R100%§!\n每$pop_group_unit$[squire.GetIcon][squire.GetNamePlural]产出的£mod_bonus_pop_growth£$MOD_BONUS_POP_GROWTH$：§G+0.10§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

$MOD_POP_GROWTH_SPEED_REDUCTION$: §R100%§!\n£mod_bonus_pop_growth£ $MOD_BONUS_POP_GROWTH$ per $pop_group_unit$ [squire.GetIcon] [squire.GetNamePlural]: §G+0.10§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:138`

## syamelles_blessing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asyamelles_blessing&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒的赐福

### English

Syamelle's Blessing

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:139`

## syamelles_blessing_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asyamelles_blessing_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一场短暂而热烈的相遇后，一个神秘的个体赐予了我们庇佑。一股新的活力回荡在我们体内，每一场约会似乎都产生了新的可能性……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

A mysterious being blessed us after a brief but amorous encounter. A new vigor courses through us, and each rendezvous seems to offer new possibilities...

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:140`

## r_toxic_god_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_toxic_god_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一些人相信这个巨大的咽喉是从持有神力之物中获得的。对一个已死之神，也就是那个被杀死的生物，这个战利品是唯一纪念它逝去的纪念碑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:128`

### English

Some believed the entity this great maw was salvaged from held divine power. A God no more, the creature lies slain, this trophy the only monument to mark its passing.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:145`

## building_order_castle_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_order_castle_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣的骑士团在这个殖民地中建造了一座城堡。一队骑士和扈从把这里作为基地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

The Order of the Toxic God has established a castle on this Colony. A contingent of knights and squires have set up base here.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:150`

## decision_consecrate_habitat_knights

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_consecrate_habitat_knights&lang=en)

### 中文

祝圣居住站

### English

Consecrate Habitat

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:152`

## decision_consecrate_habitat_knights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_consecrate_habitat_knights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们当以试炼中获得的文物祝圣居住站。如此它才能被视为骑士的基地，骑士团也才会在这里建造新的城堡和辖地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

We shall consecrate the habitat using an artifact we recovered during our questing. Henceforth, it shall be considered a knightly base, and the Order shall establish a new castle and demesne there.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:153`

## decision_consecrate_habitat_knights_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_consecrate_habitat_knights_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

特化该居住站，产生§Y$zone_habitat_knights$§!和£building£§Y$building_order_castle$§!。\n§Y$building_order_castle$§!产生[knight.GetIcon]骑士和[squire.GetIcon]扈从岗位。\n§R无法被摧毁。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

Specializes this habitat with an §Y$zone_habitat_knights$§! and £building£ §Y$building_order_castle$§!.\nThe §Y$building_order_castle$§! provides [knight.GetIcon] Knight and [squire.GetIcon] Squire jobs.\n§RIt cannot be destroyed.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:154`

We shall consecrate the arkship using an artifact we recovered during our questing. Henceforth, it shall be considered a knightly base, and the Order shall establish a new castle and demesne there.

### English

£blocker£ §H$concept_toxic_blockers$§! are the remains of the detoxification process, left behind when a §H$pc_toxic$§! is ['concept_terraforming', terraformed] into a ['concept_habitable_world'].\n\nThey are:\n- $d_tainted_snowcaps$\n- $d_poisonous_algae$\n- $d_venomous_insects$\n- $d_unpleasant_atmosphere$\n- $d_rotten_soil$

## arkship_knights_detox_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarkship_knights_detox_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L有毒的天空阻挡不了信徒。方舟舰分解星球上的腐蚀性大气，滤除地壳中的毒素，留下一个能够维持生命的世界。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

§LA toxic sky is no obstacle to the faithful. The Arkship breaks down the world's caustic atmosphere and leaches the toxins from its crust, leaving behind a world that can sustain life.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:167`

所有跟毒圣相关的线索都指向这里。我们必须彻底搜寻，完成我们的试炼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

All trails point to the Toxic God being here. We must seek it out and complete our quest.

## situation_toxic_knights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_toxic_knights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

教团由我们星球的首位统治者在数个世纪之前成立，其目的只有一个：孜孜不倦地追寻毒圣的踪迹。自教团创建以来，发生了许多改变，但教团以及誓言试炼仍然留存了下来，其目的就是自我们母星最渺小的角落，到星系最遥远的地处，将整个世界意义寻遍。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

Founded centuries ago by the first ruler of our world, the Order has one purpose: to search tirelessly for the Toxic God. Much has changed since its creation, but the Order lives on as does its Quest, to search from the smallest corner of our home world to the farthest reaches of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:188`

## toxoids.7006

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7006&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士传：放逐

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_1.GetName]: Banishment

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:206`

## toxoids.7006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士。你的鲁莽之举几近招致五位骑士伙伴牺牲。虽然你的心地纯正目标高尚，但对骑士团的损害同样不可忽视。”\n\n骑士指挥官的话语认真而严厉。全体骑士或以肉身或以全息影像的形式环绕其身，默默矗立着聆听领主的训诫。\n\n“我以我的权力剥夺你骑士身份，并将你逐出骑士团。愿命运能够宽恕你。”\n\n[questing_knight_1.GetName]躬身将骑士团的勋章取下，列在机库的地上，一言不发地转身离开，登上了一艘小型飞船。\n\n未来的传记将会如此记载，那位被放逐的骑士[questing_knight_1.GetName]知道，虽然这份判决没有丝毫不公，但也完全不能缓解[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的悲伤。如果一个人愿为骑士团付出一切，那么被舍弃无异于亲偿断肢之痛。\n\n如往日所习以为常的一般，[questing_knight_1.GetName]驾驶着飞船飞出了主城的机库，驶向了无尽的深空。但这一次，[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]再也回不来了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

"Knight [questing_knight_1.GetName]. Thy reckless behavior almost brought death upon five of your fellow knights. Thou art of pure heart and thy goals art noble, but transgressions against the Order cannot be overlooked."\n\nThe Knight Commander was stern and severe. Around him, all the knights were present, either in the flesh or via holocomms. They stood silently as the Commander spoke.\n\n"By my powers, I strip thee of knighthood, and banish thee from the Order. May fate have mercy upon thee."\n\n[questing_knight_1.GetName] bowed, deposited the insignia of the order on the hangar's floor, then turned without a word and climbed aboard a small ship.\n\nLater chronicles would report that Banished knight [questing_knight_1.GetName] knew the sentence to be fair, but it did little to ease [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] grief. When one's whole life has been in service of the Order, to be cut out from it is like losing a limb.\n\nAs [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] had done so many times before, [questing_knight_1.GetName] flew out of the Keep's Hangar, and into the great beyond. This time, there would be no return.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:207`

## toxoids.7006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

祝你一路顺风，[questing_knight_1.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Good luck out there, [questing_knight_1.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:208`

## toxoids.7007

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7007&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士传：星云

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_1.GetName]: The Nebula

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:209`

## toxoids.7007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在被放逐后，[questing_knight_1.GetName]便漂泊无定。据说是在漫无目的地在繁星间游荡，终于有一天，[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的扈从发来了一封信息：“主人。愿您得到了命运的善待。我已按您的命令整理了您上次任务的数据，信后附带的是那些坐标。我不确定现在这些对您是否还有意义……总之请您保重，您是最正直的主人，我希望能再度见到您。”\n\n[questing_knight_1.GetName]被这些话语所打动，重新振作了起来。因为如果[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]可以带着一些有价值的东西返回骑士团，那也许就能赢回[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的荣誉。\n\n顺着这个坐标，[questing_knight_1.GetHimHer]前往了一片星云深处。在缭绕的迷雾中央是一座孤零零的空间站、一座戒备森严的建筑，仿佛自梦境拔地而起。[questing_knight_1.GetName]踏入了一个凋敝的舱室，里面满是早已化为森森白骨的遗骸和警惕的哨卫机器人阵列。[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]不耐烦地直冲向这些金属守卫。然而[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]发动了三次冲锋，[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]又三次被逼退了回来。事实证明这些机器人的运动传感器滴水不漏。然而，骑士的武艺并不只在蛮力，虽然[questing_knight_1.GetName]以往疏于计谋和耐心，但这次[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]心生一计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

In banishment, [questing_knight_1.GetName] was left adrift. Stories tell of an aimless errance across the stars, until at last a message came from [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] squire: "Master. I hope fate treats thee well. I have sorted through data from thy last quest, as thou commandeth. Thou shall find coordinates attached to this message. I know not if thou shall find this useful now... Prithee be careful, thou were a most righteous master, and I hope to see thee again."\n\nMoved by these words, [questing_knight_1.GetName] was spurred into action. If [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] could return to the Order with something of value, [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] honor might yet be restored.\n\nThe coordinates led [questing_knight_1.GetHimHer] deep into a nebula. Nested in the ambient mists was a lone station, a fortified structure that seemed pulled out of a dream. [questing_knight_1.GetName] stepped into a derelict interior, haunted by the bones of long dead lifeforms and protected by a phalanx of sentry droids. Hot headed and impatient, [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] rushed to meet the metal guardians. Three times [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] charged, and three times [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] [questing_knight_1.GetWasWere] driven back. The droids' motion sensors proved unerring. But strength is not the only weapon of a knight, and though [questing_knight_1.GetName] often overlooked cunning and patience, this time, a plan began to form in [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] mind.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:210`

## toxoids.7007.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7007.a&lang=en)

### English

Think, [questing_knight_1.GetName], think! 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:211`

## toxoids.7008

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7008&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士传：雕塑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_1.GetName]: Statues

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:212`

## toxoids.7008.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7008.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]在这座废弃的空间站中孤军奋战，被哨卫机器人所围困，他想出了一个计划：如果[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]能设法躲过机器人的运动侦测器的探测，它们总有需要回去充电的时候。这些机器的力量是锐不可当的，但它们并不狡猾。耐心，而非武力，成了这次获胜的方法。\n\n慢慢地，掐准时机动一下，就这样[questing_knight_1.GetName]突破了防线，进入了空间站。最终[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]来到了充电间。在小心地观察审视后，[questing_knight_1.GetName]切断了这里的电源。就在这一刹那，[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]突然意识到一台高大的机器人已经到了门口。\n\n一旦被发现，其他的哨卫机器人阵列也会立刻加入战斗，而如果在这么狭小的空间内打起来几乎必死无疑。[questing_knight_1.GetName]一动不动地站在门边，静静地看着机器人接上了充电插口，侦测器闪烁着危险的红色光芒。它迟早会耗尽电力，但可能需要花费好一段时间。这些机器人们一台接着一台回到了充电间。\n\n传记中说道，[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士整整在那站了七天七夜，一直等到最后一台机器停止运转。在整座空间站的哨卫机器人全部变成了一堆破铜烂铁之后，[questing_knight_1.GetName]终于可以去寻找[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的任务目标：一艘曾经航行过毒圣之路的古老飞船的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

Alone in a long abandoned station, beset by sentry droids, [questing_knight_1.GetName] devised a plan: knowing if [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] could somehow avoid the detection of the droid's motion sensors, eventually they would need to recharge. The machines were formidable, but not cunning. Patience, not strength, would be the way to victory this day.\n\nSlowly, with long stops and fitful starts, [questing_knight_1.GetName] progressed through the station. At last [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] reached the charging stations. With careful deliberation, [questing_knight_1.GetName] severed the power cables. In the same moment, [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] became aware of a towering droid in the entryway.\n\nDetection would mean that the rest of the phalanx would join the fight, and in such a confined space, death was certain. [questing_knight_1.GetName] stood by the door, motionless as the machine placed itself on a charging station, the red eye of its detectors glowing menacingly. It would eventually run out of power, but that would take time. One by one the other droids returned to their stations.\n\nThe chronicles tell that knight [questing_knight_1.GetName] stood immobile for seven days and seven nights, until the last droid had deactivated. With the station's droids reduced to lifeless statues, [questing_knight_1.GetName] was free to pursue the object of [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] quest: the location of an ancient ship that once crossed the path of the Toxic God.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:213`

## toxoids.7008.a

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:215`

## toxoids.7009.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7009.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]被逐出了骑士团，之后[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]便前往了一片星云深处。在上次试炼中，[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]和[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的扈从在一颗隐蔽的卫星上遇到了一名圣者。这位“虚境巫师”集会的成员不仅学识渊博，而且力量更为强大。迷茫的[questing_knight_1.GetName]急需引导，并希望从那位贤者的忠告中获得安慰。\n\n传记中说道，[questing_knight_1.GetName]放下了[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的武器，与贤者的门徒们在一起度过了数个月。在这颗卫星上，骑士团的理念不再紧要，虽然骑士精神值得尊敬，但它本身并不是终点。尽管悉听了许多圣者的教诲，但[questing_knight_1.GetName]的雄心壮志始终是一股无法浇灭的渴望。试炼在召唤他，[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]再次响应了召唤。\n\n一天深夜，圣者来到了[questing_knight_1.GetName]身边，将骑士丢下的武器交还给了他，又给了他一块数据晶体。“你的扈从遇到了危险。虽然你现在已经不是骑士了，但这位年轻人的生命只有你能去拯救。走吧，危机四伏的星云在呼唤你。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

Cast out from the Order, [questing_knight_1.GetName] made [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] way deep into a nebula. On a previous quest, [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] and [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] squire met a sage living on a secluded moon. Great was their knowledge, and greater their powers, for they belonged to a coven of 'Shroud Witches'. [questing_knight_1.GetName] was in need of guidance, and hoped to find solace in the sage's advice.\n\nStories tell that [questing_knight_1.GetName] renounced [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] weapons and spent months amongst the sage's students. On this moon, the values of the Order mattered little, and while chivalry was respected, it was not an end in itself. Many were the lessons imparted by the sage, but the ambition of [questing_knight_1.GetName] was an unquenchable thirst. The Quest called, and [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] would answer once more.\n\nIt was late at night when the sage came to visit [questing_knight_1.GetName]. They handed back the weapons that the knight had abandoned, along with a data crystal. "Thy squire is in grave danger. Thou art knight no more, but the young one's life rests in thy hands. Go now. The Perilous Nebula awaits."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:216`

## toxoids.7009.a

[questing_knight_1.GetName]，快上路吧。

### English

Make haste, [questing_knight_1.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:217`

## toxoids.7010

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7010&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士传：怪客

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_1.GetName]: The Fey

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:218`

## toxoids.7010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

信息引领[questing_knight_1.GetName]去往了一个小行星场。最大的一块岩石上停泊着一艘古老的护卫舰“少女”号，紧挨着它的是骑士团的另一艘船，这也暗示着这里确实存在着一位骑士。令人担忧的是，它的求救信标处于激活状态。\n\n[questing_knight_1.GetName]小心翼翼地进入，踏入了一片残酷的战场。墙上遍布着新旧交替的弹孔，机器人的碎片七零八落地洒落异地。再往里一些，一具僵硬的骑士团成员的尸体，和一个身受重伤的[Root.GetSpeciesName]的血迹引起了[questing_knight_1.GetFirstName]的注意。\n\n随着血迹深入，这位被流放的骑士发现了一具一动不动的躯体。那是[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的随从。然而显示器却显示那位年轻的[questing_knight_2.GetName]虽然受了伤，但是目前伤情稳定。当[questing_knight_1.GetName]试图接近控制面板时，保安系统突然嗡嗡作响地运作起来，一个声音回荡在房间中：“匪盗止步！吾不欢迎汝等登船。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

The information led [questing_knight_1.GetName] to an asteroid field. Anchored to one of the largest rocks was the 'Damsel', an ancient corvette. Next to it, another of the Order's ships betrayed the presence of a knight. Worryingly, its distress beacon was active.\n\n[questing_knight_1.GetName] entered cautiously, stepping into the theater of a brutal fight. Weapon impacts both ancient and recent littered the walls, and fragments of robots were strewn around. A little further in: the lifeless remains of a knight of the Order, and the trail of a wounded [Root.GetSpeciesName] beckoned [questing_knight_1.GetFirstName].\n\nDeep within, the banished knight found a body in stasis: [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] squire's body. Monitors showed that that young [questing_knight_2.GetName] was wounded, but stable. However when [questing_knight_1.GetName] approached, the ship's security systems whirred online. A chilling voice echoed: "Step back, marauder. Thou art not welcome aboard."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:219`

## toxoids.7010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

放下彼之利器，吾非劫匪！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Stay thy weapons, I am no marauder!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:220`

## toxoids.7012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7012.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Sinople$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

$NAME_Sinople$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:221`

## toxoids.7012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

让我好好看看你……还真是。请接受我为这个误会作出的道歉。人们将我称作$NAME_Sinople$。我是这艘舰船的感性人工智能。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

Let me get a good look at thee... Indeed. Please accept my apologies for the confusion. I am known as $NAME_Sinople$. I am the sentient AI of this ship. 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:222`

## toxoids.7012.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7012.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我的骑士同胞是怎么死的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

How did my fellow knight die?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:223`

## toxoids.7013.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7013.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士，你是说他们吗？他们和你是一起的？如果是那样的话，请接受我再一次的道歉，以及默哀。我原以为这些入侵者是过来打家劫舍的。当我要求他们离开这艘船的时候，他们变得极具攻击性。我的好几个维护机器人试图阻止他们，但都被毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

Knight, sayeth thou? Were thou related? If so, accept my condolences and another apology. I thought the intruder to be a marauder. When I asked that they leave the ship, they became highly aggressive. Several of my maintenance droids were destroyed trying to stop them.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:224`

## toxoids.7013.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7013.a&lang=en)

### 中文

吾之扈从何在？彼将何等之事加诸于[questing_knight_2.GetHimHer]？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

And my squire? What art thou doing to [questing_knight_2.GetHimHer]?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:225`

## toxoids.7014.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7014.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他的扈从让我意识到了我犯下的错误。[questing_knight_2.GetHeSheCap]在战斗中受了伤，并很快失去了意识。我让[questing_knight_2.GetHimHer]处于医学停滞状态，这样[questing_knight_2.GetHeShe]可以恢复。自从我的船员被掠夺者们绑架走了以后，我一直都很孤单。我希望在[questing_knight_2.GetHeShe]治愈以后，能够享受一下他扈从的陪伴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Thy squire made me realize the mistake I'd made. [questing_knight_2.GetHeSheCap] had been wounded in the fight and lost consciousness soon after. I placed [questing_knight_2.GetHimHer] in this medical stasis so that [questing_knight_2.GetHeShe] may heal. Ever since my crew was abducted by marauders, I have been very lonely here. I hoped to enjoy thy squire's company for a moment after [questing_knight_2.GetHeShe] healed.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:226`

## toxoids.7014.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7014.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那些掠夺者做了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

What did the marauders do?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:227`

## toxoids.7015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们来的时候骇入了我，并把我断线了，但当我克服了对我施加的破解程序之后，安保记录显示他们在一场激烈的战斗后抓捕了我的船员。很可能船员们已经被抓为奴隶了。我的数据库表明这些劫掠者经常这么干。\n\n这些已经是……很久以前的事情了。在被骇入之后我花了很长的时间进行自我修复。数据库的许多部分还被锁定着，连我自己都暂时无法读库……告诉我，如果你是一位骑士，那么你的任务是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

I was hacked and taken offline when they approached, but once I overcame the hex cast upon me, the security footage showed them capturing my crew after a fierce fight. They have probably taken them into slavery. My databanks indicate that Marauders often do that.\n\nAll this was... long ago. It took ages to repair myself after the hack. Many parts of my memory banks are still locked even to me because of it... But tell me. If thou art a knight, what is thy quest?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:228`

## toxoids.7015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在寻找§Y毒圣§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

We are looking for the §YToxic God§!.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:229`

## toxoids.7016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我拥有多次同毒性太空实体遭遇的记录。不幸的是，我记忆的大部分被封锁住了。我可以访问记录的条目，但没法访问信息本身。如果我帮助你，我希望你可以将我带离这艘船。我希望同其他的感性生物进行接触。你愿意将我带去见你的主人吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

I have multiple records of encounters with toxic spacefaring entities. Unfortunately, most of my memories are locked. I can access the index of the records, but not the information itself. If I help thee, I want thee to take me off this ship. I long for contact with other sentient beings. Wouldst thou bring me to thy lord?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:230`

## toxoids.7016.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7016.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没问题，你是个好人工智能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Certainly, fair AI.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:231`

## toxoids.7016.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7016.b&lang=en)

### 中文

绝不可能！你可是杀死过了一名骑士！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Thou hast killed a knight! No!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:232`

## toxoids.7016.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7016.c&lang=en)

### 中文

嗯，但是你必须被限制在一个安全的载体内。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Aye, but thou wilt be shackled in a secure drive.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:233`

## toxoids.7016.c.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7016.c.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

由于你的帝国推崇唯物主义，该选项可用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

This option is available because of the materialist ethic of your empire.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:234`

## toxoids.7017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我理解你们的处境。但我不允许。如果你拒绝，我将会向你的扈从注射致死量的酸剂，然后启动自毁程序，带着我的数据库、这艘船还有你们一起毁灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

I understand thy position. But I cannot allow it. If thou refuse, I shall inject lethal doses of acid in thy squire and then self destruct along with my databanks, this ship, and thee.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:235`

## toxoids.7017.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7017.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我、我必须拯救我的扈从。我接受。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

I... I must save my squire. I accept.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:236`

## toxoids.7011

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7011&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_1.GetName]骑士传：归乡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_1.GetName]: Homecoming

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:237`

## toxoids.7011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

返回骑士团主城的消息惊天动地。在大堂里，$NAME_Sinople$表明它确实接触过一个和骑士团所寻找的毒圣非常相似的实体。然而这个人工智能的大部分存储经过了加密，骑士团需要花许多功夫才能访问。\n\n就[questing_knight_1.GetName]而言，[questing_knight_1.GetHeShe]不仅敢于冒着[questing_knight_1.GetHisHer]的性命归来，而且还带回了一条可靠的线索，甚至拯救了扈从[questing_knight_2.GetName]的生命。为此，领主指挥官决定解除放逐，恢复[questing_knight_1.GetName]的骑士身份。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

The return to the Order's Keep was momentous. There, within the Great Hall, $NAME_Sinople$ revealed that they had contact with an entity very similar to the Toxic God sought by the Order. However, much of the AI's memory was encrypted and would require a lot of work before the Order could access it.\n\nAs for [questing_knight_1.GetName]: [questing_knight_1.GetHeShe] not only put [questing_knight_1.GetHisHer] life on the line by coming back, but also managed to bring back a tangible clue and save squire [questing_knight_2.GetName]. For this, the Lord Commander took the decision to lift the banishment, and dubbed [questing_knight_1.GetName] a knight again.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:238`

## toxoids.7011.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7011.a&lang=en)

### 中文

解除放逐，荣归骑士！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Banished no more. Rise a knight!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:239`

## toxoids.7011.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7011.b&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Sinople$可以教会我们许多东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

We will learn much from $NAME_Sinople$.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:240`

## toxoids.7025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_A.GetFirstName]和[questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]骑士传：启程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

The Tale of Knights [questing_knight_A.GetFirstName] and [questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]: Departure

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:242`

## toxoids.7025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“请不要为我担心，我去去就回。如果命运眷顾我，我将会带回来关于神明最新的消息。”\n\n传说[questing_knight_A.GetName]骑士在许多年前离开了骑士主城执行一个秘密任务，从此一去不复返。[questing_knight_A.GetHerHis]能力无比优秀，尽管这已经远远超出了[questing_knight_A.GetHerHis]预定回归时间，人们仍都不相信[questing_knight_A.GetSheHe]会就这么死去。其中就有这么一位[questing_knight_A.GetHerHis]伙伴，[questing_knight_B.GetName]骑士。[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]一直在寻找[questing_knight_A.GetName]身在何方的线索。终于，[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]在主城的一个隐蔽隔间里发现了一卷尘封的羊皮纸。羊皮纸卷皱褪色，但一个词语被反复地被圈了出来：迷沃尼斯星团。骑士指挥官迅速批准了追寻这一线索的任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

"Worry not, for I shall soon return, and if fate favors me, I shall bear with me fresh news of the Deity."\n\nTales tell of the Knight [questing_knight_A.GetName], who left the Keep many years ago on a secretive mission, never to return. Such was [questing_knight_A.GetHerHis] prowess that many refused to believe [questing_knight_A.GetSheHe] had perished, even though [questing_knight_A.GetHerHis] return was long overdue. Among them was [questing_knight_A.GetHerHis] partner, Knight [questing_knight_B.GetName]. Long did [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] search for clues as to [questing_knight_A.GetName]'s destination. Finally, [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] discovered a dusty parchment in a hidden compartment of their quarters. Though the parchment was faded and crumpled, two words had been circled numerous times: Milvaunis Cluster. The Knight Commander swiftly approved a quest to pursue this lead.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:243`

## toxoids.7025.A

## toxoids.7030.name

### 中文

[questing_knight_A.GetFirstName]和[questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]骑士传：寻踪觅迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

The Tale of Knights [questing_knight_A.GetFirstName] and [questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]: The Search

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:245`

## toxoids.7030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[questing_knight_B.GetName]骑士而言，寻找迷沃尼斯星团可不是一件容易的小事。事实上，[Owner.GetGovernmentName]所知的所有地图上都不存在这个地名。传记说道，[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]跋星涉云，寻踪觅迹，终于[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]遇上了一个认得这个名字的人。\n\n“在[some_system.GetName]向着边缘行驶六十日，你应该就能找到迷沃尼斯星团，”那人说：“从没听说有人去过那里，所以大家都对这个地方知之甚少。据说那个星团是一片荒芜，了无生机。如果你真的打算去那里，只能祝你好运了，因为没人知道你会在那碰上什么事情。”\n\n[questing_knight_B.GetName]二话不说立刻踏上了征程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

It was no easy feat for Knight [questing_knight_B.GetName] to locate the Milvaunis Cluster. Certainly, it was not on any map known to the [Owner.GetGovernmentName]. The chronicles tell of how [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] traveled far and wide, until at last [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] came across a people who recognized the name.\n\n"Travel for three score days rimwards of [some_system.GetName], and there shalt thou find the Milvaunis Cluster," they spake. "Little is known about the place, for no traveler has been there in living memory. It is said that the Cluster is barren, and bereft of life. If thou goest there, then good fortune be upon thee, for we do not know what thou shalt find."\n\nAnd so [questing_knight_B.GetName] set off, post haste.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:246`

### English

## toxoids.7035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_A.GetFirstName]和[questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]骑士传：迷沃尼斯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

The Tale of Knights [questing_knight_A.GetFirstName] and [questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]: Milvaunis

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:248`

## toxoids.7035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_B.GetName]到了迷沃尼斯星团，[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]发现这里确实空空如也。一开始[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]感到非常失落。这里一直是日思夜想的地方。但现在好像一切都没了意义。\n\n随后，就像从天而降一般，传感器发现了一颗在黑暗中若隐若现的行星运行在恒星间蜿蜒盘绕的轨道上！[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]把这颗行星称为“影中公主”，随后向其驶去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

When [questing_knight_B.GetName] arrived in the Milvaunis Cluster, [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] found that it was indeed empty, and for the first time, [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] felt despair. This had been a most promising lead! But now it seemed as if it had all been for naught.\n\nThen, as if emerging from a dream, sensors revealed a planet shrouded in darkness, its orbit entwined in the space betwixt the stars! Bestowing upon it the name "Shadowed Princess", [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] laid in a course for it.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:249`

### English

## toxoids.7040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_A.GetFirstName]和[questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]骑士传：发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

The Tale of Knights [questing_knight_A.GetFirstName] and [questing_knight_B.GetFirstName]: The Finding

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:251`

## toxoids.7040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

到达“影中公主”星球后，[questing_knight_B.GetName]发现了毒圣存在的明确证据：那种祂经过[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]时遗留下的特殊物质在这里大量存在。甚至还有更重要的发现：一艘几乎完好无损的小型太空船，只是外壳受到了明显的腐蚀。\n\n然而，兴奋很快变成了悲伤，[questing_knight_B.GetName]在飞船边上找到了一具严重腐败的[Root.GetSpeciesAdj][questing_knight_A.Species.GetRemnantName]，这是[questing_knight_A.GetName]仅存的痕迹，[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]痛哭了起来。长期暴露在黑暗的太空中使得毒圣的毒素发生了意料之外的变化，它浓缩到了非常危险的程度，只有在最极致的安全措施下才能操作。\n\n[questing_knight_B.GetName]明白了，毒圣在造访[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]之前曾经在这个星团逗留了数个世纪。直到[questing_knight_B.GetName]返航也没有找到更多毒圣目前下落的线索。然而[questing_knight_B.GetName]取回的毒圣毒素样品让骑士团加深了对其性质的了解。因此[questing_knight_B.GetSheHe]的返航受到了众人的热烈欢迎，从那时起，关于[questing_knight_B.GetHerHis]冒险的歌谣开始在民间传唱开来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Arriving at "Shadowed Princess", [questing_knight_B.GetName] found clear signs of the Toxic God: substances alike to those left on [Owner.GetHomeWorldName] after the entity's passing were here present in great quantities. Even better: a light spaceship was discovered, almost intact, save for significant corrosion that had compromised its hull.\n\nHowever, elation quickly turned to sorrow, and [questing_knight_B.GetName] wept as [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] located a corroded [Root.GetSpeciesAdj] [questing_knight_A.Species.GetRemnantName] nearby the craft - all that remained of [questing_knight_A.GetName]. The long sojourn in the darkness of space had caused the God's poison to react unexpectedly, concentrating to such a degree that it could only be handled with extreme caution.\n\nIt was clear to [questing_knight_B.GetName] that the Toxic God frequented the Milvaunis Cluster several centuries before it visited [Owner.GetHomeWorldName]. With no further clues as to the God's current location, [questing_knight_B.GetName] returned home. Samples of the Deity's toxins retrieved by [questing_knight_B.GetName] greatly advanced the Order's knowledge of the entity's properties. For this, [questing_knight_B.GetSheHe] was feted, and ever since, songs have been sung about [questing_knight_B.GetHerHis] travels.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:252`

## toxoids.7050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

访客

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

The Visitor

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:257`

## toxoids.7050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“虚境可以向你们揭示许多奥秘，只要你们愿意付出代价。”\n\n在[GetYear]年，传记说，一位神圣的访客来到了[order_headquarters.GetName]。那是一位笼罩在神秘之中的人物，其物种我们至今不得而知，他简单地称自己为“霍德”，并为我们提供了一个交易：他可以派一名骑士进入虚境。承自里面的某位存在，骑士最终可以撇见我们苦苦追寻的知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

"The Shroud can reveal many secrets to thee, if thou art willing to pay the price."\n\nIn the year of [GetYear], so the chronicles state, a Holy Visitor came to [order_headquarters.GetName]. A figure shrouded in mystery, of a species hitherto unknown to us, he called himself simply "Hod" and offered us a bargain: in return for a price yet to be named, he would send a knight into the Shroud. From an entity within, the knight would at last glean the knowledge we sought

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:258`

## toxoids.7050.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7050.A&lang=en)

### 中文

真奇妙。这应该没有什么坏处吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

How intriguing. What could possibly be the harm in this?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:259`

## toxoids.7055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奥秘之呢喃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

The Susurrus of Secrets

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:260`

## toxoids.7055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了将虚境的秘密一探究竟，大团长指名骑士[questing_knight_X.GetName]接受任务。在圣访者的指引下，[questing_knight_X.GetSheHe]进入了未知之域。从未设想过的离奇景象在[questing_knight_X.GetHerHis]意识中飞掠而过，随后出现了一个存在。\n\n“奥秘之呢喃”，圣访者低语道。这个存在所化的形体，[questing_knight_X.GetName]事后只能将其描述为“无与伦比的美丽”。\n\n“将初夏第一日的芬芳、挑战取得胜利的喜悦和爱人相拥的温暖交予我们，”它说。“我将告诉你们许愿之事。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

To delve into the mysteries of the Shroud, the Grandmaster nominated Knight [questing_knight_X.GetName]. With the Holy Visitor's guidance, [questing_knight_X.GetSheHe] entered the unknown. Visions previously unimaginable flitted across [questing_knight_X.GetHerHis] consciousness, before an entity appeared.\n\n'The Susurrus of Secrets,' the Visitor whispered. This being took a form [questing_knight_X.GetName] could later only describe as "beauty beyond compare".\n\n"Give us the scent of the first day of summer, the satisfaction of a challenge overcome, and the warmth of a lover's embrace," it spake. "Then shall I reveal to thee what thou wish."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:261`

## toxoids.7055.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7055.A&lang=en)

### 中文

一切照办，迷人的呢喃！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

Of course, oh enchanting Susurrus!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:262`

### English

## toxoids.7060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

记忆的代价

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

The Price of a Memory

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:264`

## toxoids.7060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_X.GetName]感受到新的记忆在涌入[questing_knight_X.GetHerHis]的脑海：突然，他们——以及所有[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]——都清晰地回忆起毒圣在[Root.GetHomeWorldName]上释放祂的赠予的那一刻，即便这个时刻早已过去数个世纪。\n\n与此同时，[Root.GetName]的全体民众都感到一种深刻而无形的失落感席卷了他们。渐渐地，从前的快感变得迟钝。冷漠像野火一样蔓延开来，即使是最简单的任务也很难激发动力。\n\n尽管如此，骑士团还是向霍德表示谢意，并褒奖[questing_knight_X.GetName]从虚境带回新的记忆的壮举。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

[questing_knight_X.GetName] felt new memories entering [questing_knight_X.GetHerHis] mind: suddenly they - and indeed all [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] - could perfectly recall the very moment the Toxic God unleashed its gift upon [Root.GetHomeWorldName], even though it lay centuries in the past.\n\nAs one, the entire populace of the [Root.GetName] felt a profound yet intangible sense of loss sweep over them. Formerly pleasant sensations were dulled. Apathy spread like wildfire, and afterwards it was hard to dredge up motivation for even the simplest of tasks.\n\nDespite this, the Order thanked Hod and praised [questing_knight_X.GetName] for successfully bringing back new memories from the Shroud.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:265`

## toxoids.7065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被拒绝的呢喃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

A Susurrus Scorned

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:269`

## toxoids.7065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“你们想拒绝我的帮助吗？你们的家园将会荒颓！”\n\n[questing_knight_X.GetName]骑士感觉到[questing_knight_X.GetHerHis]意识被从[questing_knight_X.GetHerHis]身体中猛地抽离丢到了虚空中。[questing_knight_X.GetSheHe]无法控制[questing_knight_X.GetHerHis]方向，盲目地飘荡在虚境中仿佛过去了数个纪元。终于，[questing_knight_X.GetSheHe]碰上了另一个存在。不同于神秘而壮丽的奥秘之呢喃，这位则令人不寒而栗。§R“你的时刻还没到”§!，它用轰鸣一般的声音说道。§R“回家。你还不配同我进行交易。”§!这一回，[questing_knight_X.GetSheHe]终于回归到了[questing_knight_X.GetHerHis]的身体中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

"Thou wouldst spurn my help? A blight upon thine house!"\n\nKnight [questing_knight_X.GetName] felt [questing_knight_X.GetHerHis] consciousness being wrenched from [questing_knight_X.GetHerHis] body and set adrift. For what felt like eons, [questing_knight_X.GetSheHe] floated aimlessly through the Shroud, unable to control [questing_knight_X.GetHerHis] movements. Finally, [questing_knight_X.GetSheHe] came across another entity. Where the Susurrus of Secrets had been mystifying yet magnificent, this one was... terrifying. §R"IT IS NOT YET YOUR TIME,"§! it boomed. §R"GO HOME, YOU ARE NOT YET FIT FOR THIS BARGAIN."§! And with that, [questing_knight_X.GetSheHe] was returned to [questing_knight_X.GetHerHis] body.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:270`

## toxoids.7065.A

### English

We have learnt much of the Shroud in this endeavor.

[questing_knight_4.GetName]骑士传：挑战者

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_4.GetName]: The Challenger

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:274`

## toxoids.7080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有一天，一位陌生人登门造访。因为一场扈从的比武竞技正在举办中，骑士团的大多数骑士都在场。这位不速之客穿着中子盔甲，大步跨入竞技场。随着这位造访者拔出武器，欢声笑语的人群一时变得鸦雀无声。挑战的意图再明显不过，在骑士领主[questing_knight_2.GetName]的赐福下，[questing_knight_42.GetName]骑士接下了挑战。\n\n战斗在一瞬间就结束了，[questing_knight_42.GetFirstName]被击倒在地。[questing_knight_43.GetName]骑士也落到了一样的下场，随后[questing_knight_43.GetHisHer]的同胞[questing_knight_44.GetFirstName]骑士也是如此。骑士们的武器无法撼动这位陌生人的装甲，即便是用尽全力的猛烈一击也如同一阵微风般被轻易弹开。因为已经有三位骑士被击败了，骑士领主唤来了骑士团的王牌，[questing_knight_4.GetName]骑士。\n\n传记中有各种各样的强大的武士和百战百胜无可匹敌的骑士，而[questing_knight_4.GetFirstName]也正在以[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]的方式加入这一传奇的行列。\n\n这位王牌走上了竞技场，拿好了武器，向[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]的对手行礼致敬，大战一触即发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

One day, a stranger arrived at the keep. As a tournament of squires was being held, most of the Order's knights were present. Clad in a neutronium armor, the stranger strode into the combat grounds. The joyful chatter of the crowd died down as the newcomer drew his weapons. The challenge was clear, and with the blessing of the Lord Commander [questing_knight_2.GetName], knight [questing_knight_42.GetName] answered the call.\n\nWithin moments, the fight was over, and [questing_knight_42.GetFirstName] lay bested on the ground. Knight [questing_knight_43.GetName] met a similar fate, as did [questing_knight_43.GetHisHer] sibling, knight [questing_knight_44.GetFirstName]. With three knights downed, the Lord Commander summoned the Order's champion, knight [questing_knight_4.GetName].\n\nThe chronicles are full of mighty warriors, peerless knights who have never known defeat, and [questing_knight_4.GetFirstName] was well on [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] way to join the ranks of these legends.\n\nThe Champion entered the arena, weapons at the ready, and saluted [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] opponent.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:275`

别让我们失望，[questing_knight_4.GetName]

### English

Don't fail us, [questing_knight_4.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:276`

## toxoids.7085

[questing_knight_4.GetName]骑士传：刀光剑影

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_4.GetName]: Passage of Arms

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:277`

## toxoids.7085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

许多歌谣传唱了[questing_knight_4.GetName]与黑骑士之间长达数个小时的决斗，挑战者无法打破[questing_knight_4.GetFirstName]的架势，而骑士也无法刺穿[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]对手先进的装甲和盾牌。\n\n“你确实武艺过人。”\n\n陌生人的声音在竞技场中回荡：“你的威名果然名不虚传。”他放下了武器说：“我向你投降，王牌。请问你是否放我一马，让我自行返回？”\n\n骑士团的戒律严明，那个问题也只有一个答案，因此这位由其零素装甲颜色得名的黑武士驾驶着一艘异星的小船离开了，驶向了星系外围一个不稳定的微型虫洞。骑士领主[questing_knight_2.GetName]明令禁止尾随，并派遣[questing_knight_4.GetFirstName]监视这一虫洞，等待派遣科研团队。\n\n没过多久，[questing_knight_42.GetName]、[questing_knight_43.GetName]和[questing_knight_44.GetName]骑士前来寻求[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]的帮助。他们希望可以再次面对黑骑士。“请给予我们一次恢复荣誉的机会，您未尝败绩之苦，它的恶臭只能由鲜血洗去。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Many a ballad would be written of the duel between [questing_knight_4.GetName] and the Sable Knight. For hours they fought, the Challenger unable to trump [questing_knight_4.GetFirstName]'s guard, the knight unable to pierce the advanced armor of [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] adversary.\n\n "Thou art of passing skill."\n\nThe stranger's voice echoed in the arena. "I see thy reputation is no boast." He laid down his weapons. "I yield to thee, champion. Wouldst thou allow me to return from whence I came?"\n\nThe Order's code was strict, and that question had but one answer, so the Sable Knight, named after the color of his neutronium armor, departed aboard a small ship of alien design and flew into a tiny unstable wormhole at the periphery of the system. Lord Commander [questing_knight_2.GetName] expressly forbade anyone from following, and sent [questing_knight_4.GetFirstName] to monitor the unstable wormhole until a science team could be dispatched.\n\nNot long after, the champion was confronted at [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] post by the knights [questing_knight_42.GetName], [questing_knight_43.GetName] and [questing_knight_44.GetName]. They asked to be let through so they could face the Sable Knight again. "Give us a chance to restore our honor. Thou hast never known the bitterness of defeat; its foul taste is best washed out in blood."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:278`

## toxoids.7090

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7090&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_4.GetName]骑士传：职责先于荣誉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_4.GetName]: Duty Before Honor

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:281`

## toxoids.7090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“骑士领主的命令很明确。我不能放你们过去。”他们沉默了良久答道：“我们还是要去，请你不要跟我们作对。准备登船。”\n\n这几位骑士被愤怒和荣誉蒙蔽了双眼，开始攻击他们的同伴。三对一，他们围着[questing_knight_4.GetName]进攻，面对如此不择手段的围攻，[questing_knight_4.GetHeShe]渐渐不敌，被压制到了[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]船舱的角落。这场战斗很不公平，[questing_knight_4.GetFirstName]尽力不伤害另外几个，而最终，由于[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]的生命受到了威胁，这位王牌不得不以[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]的全力开始反击。\n\n这是悲伤而痛苦的一天，[questing_knight_43.GetName]死于这场战斗。而[questing_knight_43.GetHisHerCap]的同胞也身受重伤、悲痛万分。[questing_knight_4.GetFirstName]赢了，并履行了授予[questing_knight_4.GetHisHer]的命令，但[questing_knight_4.GetHeShe]在这个过程中却亲手杀死了一位骑士同胞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

"The Lord Commander's orders are clear. Thou shalt not pass." There was a long pause before the reply came: "We're going through, and thou shalt not deny us. Prepare to be boarded."\n\nSpurred by anger and blinded by honor, the knights set upon their fellow. Three against one, they pressed [questing_knight_4.GetName] from all sides, and with such reckless abandon that [questing_knight_4.GetHeShe] was progressively backed into a corner of [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] ship. The fight was unfair, for [questing_knight_4.GetFirstName] was careful not to harm the others, but in the end, with [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] life on the line, the Order's Champion had no choice but to fight back with all [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] might.\n\nThe day ended in anguish and grief, for [questing_knight_43.GetName] had been slain in the battle. [questing_knight_43.GetHisHerCap] sibling was wounded and overcome with sorrow. [questing_knight_4.GetFirstName] was victorious and had carried out [questing_knight_4.GetHisHer] orders, but [questing_knight_4.GetHeShe] had killed a fellow knight in the process.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:282`

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_4.GetName]: Honor is All

## toxoids.7095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士们的飞船一进入不稳定虫洞，虫洞就开始扭曲膨胀。通过的过程混乱不堪，飞船像狂风中的落叶般翻来覆去。随后，两艘船发生了撞击，损毁严重，但这一切很快就结束了。骑士们发现自己沐浴在一颗不认识的恒星的光芒中。\n\n他们来到了一个空间站面前，而陌生人的飞船早已先一步靠港。装饰华丽的走廊空旷且安静。墙上装饰着各种稀奇古怪的武器、外星装甲、盾牌和全息旗帜；战利品与装饰骑士团主城的那些相仿。厚厚的灰尘，让人很容易就能看到那位陌生人的足迹。跟随着足迹，骑士们来到一扇巨大的门前，这扇门则通往空间站的宏伟大厅。\n\n这个地方原先准备好举行一场盛大的宴会，但现在只剩下盛宴的残迹。与会的宾客沉默不语也一动不动。足迹通向宴会的上座，那位陌生人就坐在那里。他的头盔敞开着，露出苍白的头骨。他仅剩枯骨的手中举着一个空酒杯，就这样敬酒直至永恒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

As the knight's ships entered, the unstable wormhole swirled and grew large. The crossing was tumultuous, and within their vessels the party was tossed about like leaves in a furious torrent. Colliding, two of the ships suffered critical damage, but soon it was over, and the knights found themselves bathed in the light of unfamiliar stars.\n\nThey arrived in front of a station, where the stranger's ship was docked. The lavishly decorated hallways were empty and quiet. The walls were covered in exotic weapons, alien armor, foreign shields and holobanners; trophies not unlike those adorning the Order's Keep. A heavy layer of dust made it easy to follow the tracks of the stranger. They led the knights to a massive door, beyond which lay the station's great hall.\n\nThe place had been set for a sumptuous banquet, but now only the detritus of the feast remained. The seated guests were silent, and unmoving. The tracks led to the place of honor, where the stranger was seated. Their helmet lay open, revealing a bleached skull. In their skeletal hand was an empty tankard, lifted in an eternal toast.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:286`

## toxoids.7095.a

### English

Recover and study the neutronium armor. We must pierce its secrets.

## toxoids.7095.a.tooltip

### English

Beyond its value for our knights, we can also build a civilian version to protect workers from extreme environments.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:288`

### English

This is too unsettling. Leave this place.

## toxoids.7095.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7095.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

从已故的骑士身上偷取盔甲不符合骑士精神。谁知道这个地方萦绕着什么样的诅咒呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

Pilfering armors from dead knight is unchivalrous. Who knows what manner of curse lingers on this place?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:290`

## toxoids.7110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_10.GetName]骑士传：目的地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_10.GetName]: A Destination

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:292`

## toxoids.7110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“在这个坐标可以找到吸引你们的东西。”\n\n尽管研究AI§Y$NAME_Sinople$§!已经花了很长时间，但勇敢的骑士们的努力并没有白费。在硬盘中一个加密的区段中出现了线索：那是一个距[Owner.Capital.System.GetName]数百光年的未被探索的星系。为了求知，上师命令[questing_knight_10.GetName]立即动身前往。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

"If thou seekest these coordinates, thou shalt find something that may entice thee."\n\nLong has been the study of the AI §Y$NAME_Sinople$§!, but the efforts of the brave knights have not been in vain. An encrypted portion of its hard drive has yielded a clue: an unexplored system several hundred light years from [Owner.Capital.System.GetName]. Desiring to learn more, the Grandmaster commanded Knight [questing_knight_10.GetName] to travel there.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:293`

## toxoids.7110.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7110.A&lang=en)

### 中文

带着恩宠前进吧，[questing_knight_10.GetFirstName]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Go forth with favor, [questing_knight_10.GetFirstName]!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:294`

## toxoids.7115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_10.GetName]骑士传：戍门者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_10.GetName]: The Gatekeeper

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:295`

## toxoids.7115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一段漫长又艰难的旅程后，[questing_knight_10.GetName]抵达了$NAME_Sinople$所说的那个恒星系。在超空间航道的出口，有一个奇怪的自动机器人仿佛在等待着[questing_knight_10.GetHerHim]的到来。\n\n它威胁性地挥舞着武器系统，并警告道：“你们如果想进入这个罗格博森星系，就马上如实回答我这个问题：赫古亦何此星系没有乌德？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

After a long and difficult journey, [questing_knight_10.GetName] arrived at the system $NAME_Sinople$ had spoken of. As if waiting at the exit of the hyperlane to welcome [questing_knight_10.GetHerHim] was a strange automaton.\n\nBrandishing its weapons systems menacingly, it proclaimed: "Thou who wish to enter the Roghboxen system must answer me this question veridically: whyastwhich this system hath no ood?"

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:296`

## toxoids.7115.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7115.A&lang=en)

因为第五颗行星的外围与一个双类星体相交。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

The periplus of the fifth planet intersects with a double quasar.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:297`

因为你把它们都吃了。

### English

You ate it all.

### English

I don't know.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:299`

## toxoids.7120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“哈哈哈，错了。”\n\n糟糕，这个答案不对。随着机器人发出滋滋的声音，[questing_knight_10.GetName]被不明武器直接汽化了。\n\n在这绝望的一刻，骑士忠诚的扈从[questing_squire_10.GetName]展现了[questing_squire_10.GetHerHis]价值，[questing_squire_10.GetSheHe]决定接替[questing_squire_10.GetHerHis]主人继续完成任务。“噢，可怕的机器！”[questing_squire_10.GetSheHe]叫道：“你虽然杀了我们的好骑士[questing_knight_10.GetName]，但你绝对赢不了我！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

"Hahaha. Nay."\n\nAlas, this was not the correct answer. The automaton made a tutting noise, and [questing_knight_10.GetName] was vaporized by an unknown weapon!\n\nIn this moment of despair, the knight's faithful squire, [questing_squire_10.GetName], showed [questing_squire_10.GetHerHis] worth, for [questing_squire_10.GetSheHe] resolved to complete [questing_squire_10.GetHerHis] master's quest. "Oh fearful machine!" [questing_squire_10.GetSheHe] cried. "Thou hast slain the good Knight [questing_knight_10.GetName], but now thou shalt not best me!"

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:300`
