## toxoids.7125.desc1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7125.desc1&lang=en)

### 中文

“你个混蛋！别用这激光照我！算了算了，你们爱去就去吧。”\n\n$toxoids.7125.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

"Do not beset me with thy foul lasers, villain! Fine, fine, thou mayest pass - much good it will do thee."\n\n$toxoids.7125.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:306`

## toxoids.7125.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7125.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

“啊，你真聪明！这就是我系统中记录的正确答案。如果觉得这样更好的话，你们就可以通过了。”\n\n$toxoids.7125.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

"Ah, thou art wise! For that is the answer contained in my databanks. Thou mayest pass, if that doth pleaseth thee."\n\n$toxoids.7125.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:307`

## toxoids.7125.desc3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7125.desc3&lang=en)

### 中文

“啊，你太聪明了！我答不上来，可能因为答案被从我的数据库中删除了。确实，我的这个任务确实不正常……唔，如果觉得这样更好的话，你们就可以通过了。”\n\n$toxoids.7125.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

"Ah, thou art truly wise! For I cannot answer thee this question, as it has been removed from my databanks. Forsooth, this task of mine is irregular indeed... Hmm, thou mayest pass, if that doth pleaseth thee."\n\n$toxoids.7125.desc.shared$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:308`

## toxoids.7130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_10.GetFirstName]骑士传：机器人的徒弟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_10.GetFirstName]: The Robot's Disciple

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:312`

## toxoids.7130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

于是，扈从[questing_squire_10.GetName]进入了§Y罗格博森§!星系。在这里，[questing_squire_10.GetSheHe]遇上了一颗也许就在短短几个世纪以前曾经被毒圣造访过的星球，上面留下了祂的大量痕迹。\n\n[questing_squire_10.GetSheHe]泊入轨道时，他收到了地表发来的一个信号：“你好！我叫朱尔兹，我是一个亚感性人工智能，也是高贵的$NAME_Sinople$的门徒，我相信你一定认识他。按照我人工智能主人的吩咐，我已经探索了整颗行星，发现了许多关于全能虫群的信息，它们是由毒圣一度安置于此。相关的数据我正在向你传输过去。”随后收到了一系列数据和另一条信息：“我的任务完成了，特此向您道别！”在这个无人机自毁的同时，行星的表面探测到了一处能量脉冲。\n\n在[questing_squire_10.GetName]得意洋洋地返航后，[questing_squire_10.GetSheHe]的英勇得到了大家的认可，并认为值得提拔进入骑士团。除此之外，骑士团的研究单位提出了一个颇具诱惑力的提案：只需再获得不多的一些数据点，并假定毒圣依照数据的规律进行移动，他们就能三角定位出毒圣现在可能存在的一片宇域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

And so [questing_squire_10.GetName] the Squire entered the §YRoghboxen§! system. There, [questing_squire_10.GetSheHe] came upon a planet recently visited by the Toxic God - perhaps even as little as a century ago, judging by the telltale signs it left behind.\n\nAs [questing_squire_10.GetSheHe] entered orbit, a signal was picked up from the surface: "Greetings! I am Gules, sub-sentient AI and servant of the noble $NAME_Sinople$, whomst I believe thou knowest. As my designated liege AI bade me, I have surveyed this planet and discovered much about the mighty swarms with which the Toxic Entity beset this planet. This information, I shall now transmit to thee." A data transfer followed, and then another transmission: "My purpose is now complete. I bid thee farewell." An energy pulse was detected on the planet's surface as the drone self-detonated.\n\nAs [questing_squire_10.GetName] returned home, triumphant, [questing_squire_10.GetSheHe] was deemed worthy of knighthood and elevated into the Order. Furthermore, the Order's research unit raised an enticing proposition: with just a few more data points, and assuming regular movement on the Toxic God's behalf, they might be able to triangulate which region of space to search for its current location.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:313`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_6.GetName]: Guidance of the Fey

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:318`

## toxoids.7145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“骑士团派给了你一项任务，[questing_knight_6.GetName]骑士。”骑士领主用不容置疑的语气说道。“$NAME_Sinople$最后的数据库终于吐出了它的秘密，我们成功取回了可以推测毒圣运动轨迹的大量数据点。你们的使命有三：调查这些坐标；测试我们最新的装甲原型。最后，在不久前，你的一位骑士同僚在$NAME_Sinople$所说的同一个星系失踪了。第三项使命是希望你能去查明其失踪的原因。”\n\n骑士团新的中子装甲原型简直无与伦比：它是骑士团工匠精湛技艺的结晶，比以往的一切铠甲都更坚固、更轻盈。它具有强大的护盾生成器和精美的雕刻。但这都还不是最厉害的，最强的功能在于它可以直接与$NAME_Sinople$联络，使其使用者在星系的任何地方都能获得这位人工智能指引。有了这件装甲，[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]感觉自己无人可挡！\n\n这趟旅途平淡无奇，但在登陆目标行星后不久，他遇到了一个穿着红色斗篷的陌生人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

"The Order has a mission for thee, knight [questing_knight_6.GetName]." The Lord Commander's voice was imperious. "$NAME_Sinople$'s last databank has finally given up its secrets, and we have retrieved enough data for a speculative model of the Toxic God's trajectory. Thy mission shall have three parts: first, thou art to investigate these coordinates; second, shalt thou test our prototype armor; finally, some time back, one of thy fellow knights disappeared at the very coordinates now provided by $NAME_Sinople$. It is your task to discover what transpired there."\n\nThe Order's prototype neutronium armor was magnificent; a testament to the skill of its artisans, it was both sturdier and lighter than ever before. Its powerful shield generator was adorned with beautiful engravings. But these were mere trivialities compared to its greatest feature: the armor could now provide a direct connection to $NAME_Sinople$, allowing the AI to provide guidance from anywhere in the galaxy. With this, [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] felt ready for anything!\n\nThe travel was uneventful, but soon after landing on their target planet, they met a stranger cloaked in red.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:319`

## toxoids.7150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

请大人高抬贵手，我不过是一个需要帮助的人畜无害的朝圣者。我因为信仰毒圣，一位最神圣的神祇，而遭到一群无情家伙的迫害和追杀。我向您寻求庇护，让我可以继续和平的朝圣之旅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Have mercy milord, I am naught but a peaceful pilgrim in need of aid. Persecuted for my belief in the Toxic God, an Entity Most Holy, I find myself hunted by a relentless foe. I beg of thee, protect me and allow me to continue my pilgrimage in peace.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:322`

## toxoids.7150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7150.a&lang=en)

### English

## toxoids.7156.desc

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:338`

## toxoids.7157.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7157.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我在星系中漫游，寻找神祇曾造访过的星球。我试图从它身后留下的腐烂荣光中寻找它的动向；这个星球曾经在被我们的神明造访后充满了光荣的有毒生命。唉，然而在这些不愿见到圣遗物的异端们的摧残下，这里就只剩下被炸碎的岩石了。我希望有朝一日，在朝圣之旅结束时，我能找到神明，并沐浴在它的恶毒光辉中！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

Wandering the galaxy, I search for worlds the deity hast visited, and I tryeth to fleer out its motives from the putrid glory left in its wake; this world wast once teeming with glorious noxious life. Alas, it is now naught but a blasted rock, sterilized by those who are not fit to see the beauty of the God's legacy. One day, when my pilgrimage is done, I hope to find the god and bask in its mephitic splendor!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:339`

## toxoids.7158.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7158.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个亵渎神明的不信者！一个狂热的杀人犯！一个曾经摧毁这个美丽的地方的人留下的守卫！在识别出我携带的遗物以及它们与瘟疫之神的联系后，它试图杀死我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

A blasphemous recusant! A zealous murderer! A Guardian, left behind by those who destroyed this once beautiful place! It tried to kill me after identifying the relics I bear and their connection to the Pestilent One.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:340`

## toxoids.7159.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7159.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有过。那是悲伤的一天。他曾同意帮助我，祝福他高贵的灵魂。唉，然后悲剧就发生了，他被追杀我的人杀死了，而我又一次侥幸逃生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

I did, yes. A sorrowful day. They at once agreed to help me, bless their noble soul. Alas, tragedy struck, for they were slain by my pursuer, and I was left fleeing once again.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:341`

## toxoids.7160

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7160&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_6.GetName]骑士传：骗子

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_6.GetName]: The Trickster

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:342`

## toxoids.7160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“哦我高贵的骑士老爷，如果您愿意为我而战，就请您收下这枚小小的护身符。它的全息影像场将会帮您模糊自己的身影，让您的对手更难以击中您。”\n\n骑士收下了，并做好了战斗的准备。[questing_knight_6.GetHisHer]的敌人身着重甲，挥舞着硕大无比的相位刃。护盾嘎吱作响、装甲火星四溅，[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]有惊无险地躲开了致命一击。[questing_knight_6.GetHisHerCap]敌人仿佛对骑士团的战斗方式了如指掌。\n\n[questing_knight_6.GetName]的装甲结结实实地吃上了一记重击，将护符制造的掩盖[questing_knight_6.GetHisHer]身形的全息影像击碎，骑士被打倒在地。就在[questing_knight_6.GetHisHerCap]的对手要发起最后一击时，突然吃了一惊般在空中凝滞住了。[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]抓住机会发起了反击，并且瞬间击穿了对方的装甲，果断地一击毙命。\n\n“太好了，太棒了！我这辈子从来没有感到这么高兴过！”这位陌生人欢呼雀跃得让人感到有点怪异。然后，[questing_knight_6.GetName]转身看向被[questing_knight_6.GetHisHer]击倒的对手。那具尸体的铠甲闪烁着光芒，覆盖在它身上的全息投影渐渐消去。出现在[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]面前的是一具骑士团成员的遗体。“哦天哪~”陌生人讪笑道，“看起来你好像杀了一个你们的骑士呀。真不幸真不幸！”陌生人的身影消失在一团紫色的烟雾中，只留下最后一句话在空中回荡。\n\n[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]跪倒在[questing_knight_6.GetHisHer]同志的遗体边上，懊恼于被全息影像愚弄。在他的铠甲里装有一处存储单元，[questing_knight_6.GetHisHer]发现了一个不寻常的东西，那是一个储存了关于毒之海的海量信息的数据库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

"O noble knight, if thou art to fight for me, please accept this charm. Its holographic field will help blur thy shape and make it harder for thine opponent to hit thee."\n\nThe knight took it, then readied for battle. Wielding a colossal phase blade, [questing_knight_6.GetHisHer] foe was heavily armored. Shields sparkled, armor was battered and [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] only narrowly avoided beheading. [questing_knight_6.GetHisHerCap] opponent somehow knew the Order's fighting style well.\n\nA hard hit landed on [questing_knight_6.GetName]'s armor, shattering the holographic projector that had concealed [questing_knight_6.GetHisHer] shape. The knight was sent tumbling to the ground. [questing_knight_6.GetHisHerCap] opponent prepared to strike the final blow, but froze mid air, as if surprised. Taking advantage of this moment of confusion, [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] pounced, slicing through the opponent's armor. A killing blow.\n\n"Oh wonderful, splendid! Never have I had such excitement in mine own life!" The stranger's cheers were loud and strangely out of character. [questing_knight_6.GetName] turned to look at [questing_knight_6.GetHisHer] defeated opponent: their armor shimmered, and the holographic skin that had masked their true form was dispelled. In front of [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] lay the remains of a knight of the Order. "Oh nay," the stranger laughed. "It seemeth that thou hath slain one of thy fellow knights! How unfortunate!" The stranger's mocking words echoed as they disappeared in a cloud of purple smoke.\n\n[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] knelt beside [questing_knight_6.GetHisHer] fallen comrade, anguished at having been duped by the stranger's holograms. In a storage unit affixed to their armor, [questing_knight_6.GetHisHer] discovered something unusual - a databank filled with wondrous secrets about the workings of the toxic seas.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:343`

## toxoids.7160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我以骑士团之名发誓，我们一定会找到你报仇雪恨！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

Upon my word, the Order's vengeance shall find thee!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:344`

## toxoids.7160.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7160.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

这里曾经是颗海洋星球，而数据库中记录了从有毒海洋中开采异星天然气的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

This world used to be oceanic, and the databanks reveal how poisonous seas can be exploited to harvest exotic gases.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:345`

## toxoids.7164.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7164.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

哈哈哈，看来我低估了你的敏锐。没关系，我自己就能轻松对付你。看你和你的骑士同胞战斗也许会很有趣，但最终的结果还是一样的。我能看到你的未来，你的死期马上就要到了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Hahaha! It seems I underestimated thine acumen. No matter, I'll simply deal with thee myself. Watching thee fight thy fellow knight would have been entertaining, but in the end, the result will be the same. I can see your future, and it's going to end very soon!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:346`

## toxoids.7164.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7164.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是这样吗？你不像我之前遇到的上一个骑士那么傻。虽然我很期待看到你们互相厮杀，但我感觉自己今天的预言不会灵验了。不管怎样，这改变不了你们很快就会死去的事实！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

Is that so? Then thou art less a fool than other knights I've known. I looked forward to watching thee fight each other, but I suppose my prediction won't come to pass. Regardless, it doesn't change that thou shall soon both be dead!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:347`

## toxoids.7164.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7164.a&lang=en)

### 中文

叛徒！你这恶魔！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Traitor! En garde, fiend!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:348`

## toxoids.7165

### 中文

[questing_knight_6.GetName]骑士传：真面貌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_6.GetName]: The True Visage

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:349`

## toxoids.7165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

朝圣者抛下了伪装的外衣，抽出两把弧光弯刀。刀刃相触碰时迸发出的刺眼红光照亮了四周。仅仅只是试探性的短兵相接也足以发现他的战斗力之超群，他仿佛在戏弄[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]骑士一般悠然自得。\n\n“悲哀的蠢货，你真觉得自己有胜过我的可能吗？我可以在你的身体还没开始做出动作时便从意识中读出你的一举一动……”紧随着的一阵残暴攻击把骑士的护盾生成器过载了。那人沾沾自喜地说：“虚境是获得许多能力的捷径。其中的一些甚至是虚境漫游者集会里教导过我的老家伙们都没搞懂的。他们仅仅只是了解了一些皮毛，而不敢去获取真正的力量！”[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]骑士抓住空档猛冲了上去，然而对方微微一笑弹开了他的攻击。“他们怎敢驱逐我，驱逐本大人！”\n\n就在此刻，$NAME_Sinople$的声音从骑士的铠甲中回荡了起来。“英勇的骑士，你的对手可以读取你的心智，但读取不了我的回路。把你的武器交于我，让我来会会他。”于是[questing_knight_6.GetFirstName]放松了全身，让$NAME_Sinople$操纵起了铠甲。\n\n战斗很快就结束了。$NAME_Sinople$猛烈地攻击对方下盘，避开了对方的格挡。对手震惊得猛吸了一口气并被击倒在地。[questing_knight_6.GetName]捡起[questing_knight_6.GetHisHer]对手的激光刃，但当[questing_knight_6.GetSheHe]要去检查对方的尸体时，对方却消失得无影无踪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

The stranger removed their cloak, and drew two curving blades of arcing light. A red glow flooded the field as blade met blade. But after only a brief skirmish, the stranger's superiority became evident. They were obviously toying with [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName].\n\n"Pathetic fool. Didst thou really think thou had a chance? I can read thine attacks in thy mind before thy body has a chance to move..." A series of brutal attacks followed, leaving the knight's shield generator depleted. In a gloating tone, the trickster continued: "The Shroud is a pathway to many abilities. Something that the decrepit Shroudwalkers who trained me never understood. They barely scrape the surface, too afraid to dive deep into their rightful powers!" Knight [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] lunged forwards, but the stranger batted away the attack with disdainful ease. "To think that they would banish me. ME!"\n\nAt that moment, $NAME_Sinople$'s voice echoed in the knight's armor. "Brave Knight, thine opponent might be able to read thy mind, but not my circuits. Surrender thine armor to me. Let me fight for thee." [questing_knight_6.GetFirstName] relaxed as $NAME_Sinople$ took over.\n\nThe fight was over in moments. $NAME_Sinople$ struck a low merciless blow, under the stranger's parry. Gasping in surprise, the stranger fell to the ground. Triumphantly, [questing_knight_6.GetName] picked up [questing_knight_6.GetHisHer] foe's luminous blades. As [questing_knight_6.GetSheHe] did so, the body of the trickster abruptly dispersed in a plume of vivid mist.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:350`

## toxoids.7165.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7165.a&lang=en)

我们还会再见面的，毫无疑问。

### English

We shall meeteth again, no doubt.

## toxoids.7165.a.tooltip

These laser weapons could make handy tools too.\n\n§Y[knight.GetNamePlural]§! will now increased the production of £alloys£ §YAlloy§! across our empire.

[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]骑士传：林中女巫

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]: The Witch of the Woods

## toxoids.7195.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7195.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事情是这样的，在[questing_knight_15.GetName]这位默默无闻、不受待见的骑士的旅途中，[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]偶然发现了在恒星之间的空间里、碎片场发出的信号。[questing_knight_15.GetSheHeCap]很快设定好了航线，前去近距离调查。\n\n在[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]接近信号源的时候，[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]听到了一个非常美妙的嗓音，穿越虚空发出了呼唤：“英勇的骑士啊！我已经在这等待了你一千年，等待你前来我的星际森林。既然你来了，我希望你能抽空光临寒舍。我保证你一定会不虚此行。”\n\n一座小型的空间站仿佛从虚空中显现。长年的碌碌无为之下，此刻[questing_knight_15.GetHerHim]非常地激动，迫不及待地想去碰碰运气，[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]立刻驾驶着[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]飞船停靠了上去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

It came to pass that during the travels of Knight [questing_knight_15.GetName] - an otherwise obscure and disfavored knight - [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] happened upon a signal emanating from a debris field in the space between stars. [questing_knight_15.GetSheHeCap] speedily laid in a course to investigate closer.\n\nAs [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] neared the source, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] heard a beautiful voice, calling from across the ether: "Oh brave knight! I have waited for thee for millennia, hoping that thou wouldst come to my celestial forest. Now that thou art here, I hope that thou wilt spare me the time for a visit to mine own humble abode. I promise yond thou shall findeth it worthwhile."\n\nA small station had appeared as if from nowhere. Impetuously - the years of fruitless searching having made [questing_knight_15.GetHerHim] impatient for a stroke of fortune - [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] moved [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] craft to dock on it.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:357`

## toxoids.7195.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7195.A&lang=en)

小心点，[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]！这可能是个陷阱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Be cautious, [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]! This could be a trap.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:358`

[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]骑士传：进入小站

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]: Entering the Station

## toxoids.7200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒，旅人之灵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

Syamelle, Spirit of Wayfarers

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:360`

## toxoids.7200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_15.GetName]骑士进入空间站时，迎接[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]的[questing_knight_15.GetWasWere]一位女性般的、美到无与伦比的存在，她看起来像[Owner.Species.GetName]，却毫无疑问地远胜于此。她露出了微笑，直至此刻，[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]才意识到此前[questing_knight_15.GetHerselfHimself]对吸引力的理解是多么地浅薄，多么地缺乏想象力。[questing_knight_15.GetSheHeCap]感受到一股无法控制的情欲在[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]内心中滋长。\n\n$toxoids.7200.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

As Knight [questing_knight_15.GetName] boarded the station, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] [questing_knight_15.GetWasWere] greeted by a feminine being of incredible beauty - she looked like a [Owner.Species.GetName], and yet there was no mistake that she was an entity far more advanced. She smiled, and [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] realized [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] concept of attractiveness had been limited - even constrained - until now. [questing_knight_15.GetSheHeCap] felt an uncontrollable lust growing within [questing_knight_15.GetHerselfHimself].\n\n$toxoids.7200.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:361`

## toxoids.7200.desc.synth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7200.desc.synth&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_15.GetName]骑士进入空间站时，迎接[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]的[questing_knight_15.GetWasWere]一位女性般的、美到无与伦比的存在，她看起来与[Owner.Species.GetName]相似，但却是有机体，而且毫无疑问远胜于此。她露出了微笑，直至此刻，[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]才意识到此前[questing_knight_15.GetHerselfHimself]对吸引力的理解是多么地浅薄，多么地缺乏想象力。即便在[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]机械躯壳内，[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]也感受到一股无法控制的情欲在[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]内心中滋长。\n\n$toxoids.7200.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

As Knight [questing_knight_15.GetName] boarded the station, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] [questing_knight_15.GetWasWere] greeted by a feminine being of incredible beauty - she looked similar to a [Owner.Species.GetName], yet curiously organic, and there was no mistake that she was an entity far more advanced. She smiled, and [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] realized [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] concept of attractiveness had been limited - even constrained - until now. Even in [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] synthetic form, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] felt an uncontrollable lust growing within [questing_knight_15.GetHerselfHimself].\n\n$toxoids.7200.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:362`

## toxoids.7200.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7200.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

“我叫叙阿美勒，旅人与流浪者之灵。我还不确定你是否值得……”她拉长了调子说道，随后上下打量了[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]一番，又说“是的，绝对值得。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

"I am Syamelle, spirit of wayfar'rs and vagabonds. I has't deem'd thee w'rthy," she drawled. She looked [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] up and down. "Aye, w'rthy forsooth."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:363`

## toxoids.7205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒又一次微笑了起来，显得那么动人，随后又脱去衣物：“我希望你能与我一同进行一场热烈的仪式，与我产出优良的子嗣。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

Syamelle smiled again, invitingly this time, and shed her clothing. "I wisheth to partake in amorous rites with thee so we can maketh many fine offspring."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:368`

## toxoids.7205.desc.no_clothes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7205.desc.no_clothes&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒又一次微笑了起来，显得那么动人，她踱向[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]的身边，牵起[questing_knight_15.Species.GetHandName]说：“我希望你能与我一同进行一场热烈的仪式，与我产出优良的子嗣。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Syamelle smiled again, invitingly this time, stepping closer to take [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] by the [questing_knight_15.Species.GetHandName]. "I wisheth to partake in amorous rites with thee so we can maketh many fine offspring."

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:369`

## toxoids.7210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_15.GetName]拿取[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]武器时，叙阿美勒的眼中闪烁出怒火：“你胆敢拒绝我！我诅咒你与你的人民：你们将永远得不到爱人的拥抱，得不到情投意合的爱情，你们将沦为繁殖的机械。在我改变主意杀了你们之前，立刻从我眼前消失！”\n\n她拿起了一个[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]之前没有注意到的装置，然后突然就消失了——尽管[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]还能感觉到她的存在，她在注视着，鄙视着……\n\n传记之后会将这件事记录为[Root.GetAdj]历史上的一个转折点：女巫的诅咒某种意义上转变成了一种庇佑，从此以后骑士们再也不会受到凡尘肉欲的影响，而机械性的繁衍相比起传统的方式并无落差，只是形式不同罢了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

As [questing_knight_15.GetName] reached for [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] weapon, Syamelle's eyes flashed with anger. "Thou wouldst spurneth me! I curse thee and thy people: thou shalt never know the embrace of a lover, nor the fiery lust of true desire; thou shalt be forced to procreate as machines do. Now begone! Before I changeth mine own mind and killeth thee instead."\n\nShe took ahold of a device that [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] had not noticed before, and suddenly she was not there - though [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] could feel her presence, watching, despising...\n\nThe chronicles would later record this as a turning point in [Root.GetAdj] history: the witch's curse was in some ways a blessing, for no longer would the knights be distracted by carnal desires, and mechanical reproduction of the populace could be just as effective as biological reproduction, in its own way.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:375`

## toxoids.7210.desc.synth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7210.desc.synth&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_15.GetName]拿取[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]武器时，叙阿美勒的眼中闪烁出怒火：“你胆敢拒绝我！看来我早该知道，不该对一台抛弃了同情心和自由以换取一套简陋金属外壳的机器有什么期待！嗨！我拿你们的金属脑子没有办法，否则我将会诅咒你们所有人，直到永远！在我改变主意拆了你们之前，立刻从我眼前消失！”\n\n她拿起了一个[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]之前没有注意到的装置，然后突然就消失了——尽管[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]还能感觉到她的存在，她在注视着，鄙视着……\n\n传记之后不知道如何评价[questing_knight_15.GetName]的经历。但是他们同意这确实是一个相当有趣的故事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

As [questing_knight_15.GetName] reached for [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] weapon, Syamelle's eyes flashed with anger. "Thou wouldst spurneth me! I shouldst has't known bett'r - for what more couldst I expecteth from a machine who is't hast abandoned all compassion and freedom in exchange f'r a st'rile suit of metal. Alas! I have no power over thee, or I wouldst lay a curse over thee, h're and now. So begone, bef're i chooseth to killeth thee instead."\n\nShe took ahold of a device that [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] had not noticed before, and suddenly she was not there - though [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] could feel her presence, watching, despising...\n\nThe chronicles didn't really know what to make of [questing_knight_15.GetName]'s tale, later. But they did agree that it made a mighty fine story.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:376`

## toxoids.7215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随后发生的事情无法描述，[questing_knight_15.GetName]对此三缄其口。在仪式结束之后，[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]注意力停留在了一个奇怪的装置上。它周围的空气似乎在闪烁着千变万化的图案，虽然很小，但却散发出一种浩瀚的气息。[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]确定[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]从来没有见过这样的东西。\n\n叙阿美勒躺下睡着了。[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]犹豫了起来。直觉告诉[questing_knight_15.GetHerHim]，[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]必须抓紧时间，趁着叙阿美勒还没醒来赶紧逃走。尽管她看上去很美好，但[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]还是感觉到了在她的表面下沉睡着巨大的愤怒。如果[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]能够带回去一些令人意想不到的东西，因[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]长期以来无所事事而对[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]不屑一顾的骑士团一定会对[questing_knight_15.GetHerHim]刮目相看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

What followed defies description, and [questing_knight_15.GetName] would later refuse to speak of it. After the conclusion, [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] view fell upon a curious device. The air seemed to shimmer in ever-changing patterns around it, and though it was quite small, it somehow gave off an aura of vastness. [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] was sure that [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] had never seen anything like it before.\n\nSyamelle lay asleep. [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] hesitated. Some instinct told [questing_knight_15.GetHerHim] that [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] should leave now, quickly, before Syamelle awoke. Despite her fairness, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] sensed a great wrath in her, slumbering below the surface. And yet, the knightly order - which for so long had treated [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName] with scorn, during [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] long and fruitless search - would surely praise [questing_knight_15.GetHerHim] highly if [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] returned with a mysterious object to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:381`

### 中文

[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]骑士传：抓个正着

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]: Caught in the Act

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:386`

## toxoids.7220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“你背叛我！你既然来到我的身边与我共枕，为什么还要偷我的东西！你个恶棍！我曾想要庇佑你，因为你的表现非常的出色……但现在，你走吧！对了，你也可以把那个装置拿走，这已经毫无用处了。快消失吧！”\n\n[questing_knight_15.GetName]以[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]最快的速度一溜烟儿跑了。而且骑士们回到了主城之后，发现这个装置绝不是毫无用处的，它可以通过某种未知的方式操纵整个维度。而[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]总是幻想，如果[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]只屈从于诱惑一次，到底[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]会得到什么样的庇佑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

"Deceit! Thou wouldst cometh h're and seduce me, and then stealeth from me! Villain! I hadst thought to blesseth thee, for thy performance was most pleasing, but now... leaveth! Aye, thou may taketh yond device, tis almost w'rthless, just begone!"\n\n[questing_knight_15.GetName] left as quickly as [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] could. And though the knights back at the keep would find the device to be the very opposite of worthless - for by unknown means it could manipulate the very dimensions - [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] would always wonder what blessing [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] may have received, had [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] only given in to temptation once.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:387`

### 中文

[questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]骑士传：叙阿美勒的赐福

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

The Tale of Knight [questing_knight_15.GetFirstName]: Syamelle's Blessing

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:392`

## toxoids.7225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒的眼睛突然睁开，说道“心爱的骑士！虽然很遗憾，但是我们分别的时候到了，你应该回到你的城堡，而我也该回去我的维度了。记住，不管你去到哪里，我的庇佑都将伴随你和你的人民们直到永远。”\n\n[questing_knight_15.GetName]返回[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]飞船时，[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]感受到了一股全新的活力在[questing_knight_15.GetHerHim]的身上流淌。不仅如此，整个[Root.GetName]似乎都感受到了。传记颇为淫猥地记载了在那一年有异常多的[Root.GetSpeciesSpawnNamePlural]降生于世。\n\n这一趟确实不虚此行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

Syamelle's eyes shot open. "Fair knight! I grieve that we must now part ways, for thou must go back to thy castle, and I to mine own dimension. But know that wh'rev'r thou goest, thou - and thy people - do go with mine own blessing."\n\nAs [questing_knight_15.GetName] boarded [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] craft, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] felt a new vigor coursing through [questing_knight_15.GetHerHim]. And indeed, the entirety of the [Root.GetName] seemed to feel this. The chronicles would somewhat smuttily record that, that year, a particularly large cohort of [Root.GetSpeciesSpawnNamePlural] were born.\n\nIt was time well spent indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:393`

## toxoids.7225.desc.synth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7225.desc.synth&lang=en)

### 中文

叙阿美勒的眼睛突然睁开，说道“心爱的骑士！虽然很遗憾，但是我们分别的时候到了，你应该回到你的城堡，而我也该回去我的维度了。记住，不管你去到哪里，我的庇佑都将伴随你和你的人民们直到永远。”\n\n[questing_knight_15.GetName]返回[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]飞船时，[questing_knight_15.GetSheHe]感受到了一股全新的活力在[questing_knight_15.GetHerHis]回路中流淌。不仅如此，整个[Root.GetName]似乎都感受到了。传记颇为淫猥地记载，许多人渴望他们物种以前的血肉之躯：每个人似乎都更加迷恋对方，而久违的原始本能又重新浮出水面，令许多人欢欣鼓舞。\n\n这一趟确实不虚此行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Syamelle's eyes shot open. "Fair knight! I grieve that we must now part ways, for thou must go back to thy castle, and I to mine own dimension. But know that wh'rev'r thou goest, thou - and thy people - do go with mine own blessing."\n\nAs [questing_knight_15.GetName] boarded [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] craft, [questing_knight_15.GetSheHe] felt a new vigor coursing through [questing_knight_15.GetHerHis] circuits. And indeed, the entirety of the [Root.GetName] seemed to feel this. The chronicles would somewhat smuttily record that, that year, many yearned for their species' formerly organic bodies: everyone seemed more enamored with each other, and primal instincts long laid to rest resurfaced anew, to the glee of many.\n\nIt was time well spent indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:394`

传奇归来

### English

A Legend Has Returned

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:397`

## toxoids.7255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“在遭到欺骗和背叛后，[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]终于死于黑暗巫师的手中。[questing_knight_8.GetHeShe]被紫色的烟雾淹没，从此消失无踪。在那一天，我们失去了最温柔的骑士，同时也失去了我们骑士团的创始人之一。这就是[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]骑士故事的结尾。”\n\n几个世纪前，传记记录了[questing_knight_8.GetName]骑士的牺牲，他协助创立了骑士团，并见证了毒圣在我们星球的降临。而今天，这部传记需要加上一段附录。因为，[questing_knight_8.GetName]回来了。骑士团调查了位于[Root.Capital.GetName]上的一处能量峰值，然后发现了刚刚从跨次元旅行返回的[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]。[questing_knight_8.GetHeSheCap]被谨慎地带回了骑士团，以便后续进一步查验[questing_knight_8.GetHisHer]的身份，并且告诉[questing_knight_8.GetHimHer]在[questing_knight_8.GetHeShe]……“去世”的这段时间里发生了些什么事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

" ... Deceived and betrayed, [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName] met [questing_knight_8.GetHisHer] fate at the hands of a dark wizard. Engulfed in purple smoke, [questing_knight_8.GetHeShe] disappeared, never to be seen again. On yon day, we lost a most gentle knight, one of the founders of the Order. Here endeth the history of knight [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]."\n\nCenturies ago, chroniclers wrote about the death of knight [questing_knight_8.GetName], who helped create the Order and witnessed the Toxic's God visit to our world. Today however, an addendum to the chronicles will have to be written, for [questing_knight_8.GetName] has returned. The Order investigated an energy spike on [Root.Capital.GetName], and met [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName], freshly returned from interdimensional travel. [questing_knight_8.GetHeSheCap] has been discreetly taken in by the Order who will verify [questing_knight_8.GetHisHer] identity and tell [questing_knight_8.GetHimHer] what has happened while [questing_knight_8.GetHeShe] was... Away.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:398`

## return_of_the_knight_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Areturn_of_the_knight_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一名创立教团的骑士，在长达一个世纪的漫长消失之后，在所有人都觉得不可能的情况下返回了。这在我们帝国内掀起一场狂欢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

The impossible return of a knight who founded the Order, after a centuries long disappearance, has caused much rejoicing within our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:402`

## toxoids.7260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7260.name&lang=en)

造访传奇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

Visiting a Legend

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:403`

## toxoids.7260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

祝安！我是扈从[questing_knight_82.GetFirstName]。骑士[questing_knight_8.GetName]希望同诸位会面。[questing_knight_8.GetHeSheCap]现在已经随时准备好接受我们的质询，并告诉我们发生了什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Greetings! I am squire [questing_knight_82.GetFirstName]. Knight [questing_knight_8.GetName] would like to meet with thee. [questing_knight_8.GetHeSheCap] stand[questing_knight_8.GetSXX] ready to face our questions, and tell us what hath happened.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:404`

## toxoids.7260.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7260.a&lang=en)

### 中文

需要事先知道什么吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Anything to know beforehand?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:405`

## toxoids.7265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[questing_knight_8.GetHeSheCap]被带去推动我们科技方面的进步，并且看上去配合得很好，但是[questing_knight_8.GetHisHer]的思想跟常人很不一样。我们的核心价值观还是相似的，但是[questing_knight_8.GetHeShe]有时候会……发表一些我们的社会演化方式的建议。最好现在回避这些话题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

[questing_knight_8.GetHeSheCap] hast been brought up to speed with our technological progress, and seemeth to be acclimating well, but [questing_knight_8.GetHisHer] mindset is very different. Our core values art similar, but [questing_knight_8.GetHeShe] sometimes have... stout opinions about the way our society hast evolved. Best avoid these topics for now.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:406`

## toxoids.7265.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7265.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该马上会见[questing_knight_8.GetHimHer]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

We shalt see [questing_knight_8.GetHimHer] anon.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:407`

## toxoids.7270.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7270.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$toxoids.7260.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

$toxoids.7260.name$

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:408`

## toxoids.7270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

终于！你们最后敢来见我了。这么说现在尔等就相当于王了……我带来了有关毒圣的重要信息，仅限传达给王。我猜尔等肯定有很多问题。现在问吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Finally! At last you deign to visit me. So thou art what passes for a king these days... I possess important knowledge about the Toxic God, meant strictly for the King. I expect thou hath many questions. Ask them.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:409`

## toxoids.7270.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7270.a&lang=en)

### 中文

汝现在所住是否舒适？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

How art thou settling in?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:410`

## toxoids.7270.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7270.b&lang=en)

### 中文

那些有关祂的传说是真的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Art all the tales about thee truthful?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:411`

## toxoids.7270.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7270.c&lang=en)

### 中文

汝的……流放期间，都发生了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

What happened during thy... exile?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:412`

## toxoids.7270.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7270.d&lang=en)

### 中文

汝言，汝可以帮助我们找到毒圣，是真的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Thou sayeth that thou couldst help findeth the Toxic God?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:413`

## toxoids.7275.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7275.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

改变得地方太多了。我以为我是回到了家，但我唯一意识到的就是我的家早已不复存在。所有我认识的人都早已逝去，只有历史宗卷能够告知我发生了什么。我认不出这个星球，但是至少骑士团依旧存在，誓言试炼仍在继续，那么我的职责就再清楚不过了。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

So much hath changed. I bethought I was returning home, only to realize yond home doesn't existeth anymore. Everyone I kneweth is long dead, and only the chronicles can enlighten me as to what hath occurred. I recognize not this world, but at least the order is still there, the quest goeth on, and mine own duty is clear. 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:414`

## toxoids.7280.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7280.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管传记非常地忠于事实，但也有一些例外，因为这些绅士们大多只会让我们的……“任务报告”读起来更顺畅。嗨，我确实杀了一百个恶魔，也为一位少女的心历尽艰险。但现在都不重要了，那位女士和其他人一样也已经不在了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

The chronicles art thruthful, there art minor variations, but for the most part those gents only maketh our... 'mission reports' more readable.  Aye, I didst slayeth a hundred fiends, and braved many dangers for the heart of a maid. Not that it matters in the present day; the lady is as dead as the others.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:415`

## toxoids.7285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我不确定。你的人们告诉我，我去到了另一个维度。对我来说，这只是一场持续几年昏睡不醒的梦。无论是我的感官还是我的感受，一切都虚无缥缈。让我挺过磨难的是对我所认识的人的回忆：我爱的、我不爱的、把我送去的那的巫师、以及我自己的愤怒；特别是那个掌握我心门之钥的人。那简直是一篇混乱，但到了最后我也获得了一些宝贵的洞见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

I'm not sure. Thy people toldeth me I hadst traveled to another dimension. To me, it was like a years long dream with no awakening. Nothing made sense, neither what I perceived, nor my own feelings. All that saw me through the ordeal were memories - recollections of people I have known: those I loved, and those I did not; the wizard who sent me there, as well as mine own wrath; and most especially, the one who holds the key to my heart. It was a chaos, but in the end I gleamed some precious insight in the process too.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:416`

## toxoids.7290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我被流放的期间，我见到了许多场景。就像照着一面破碎的镜子一般，相似的场景一遍又一遍地重复，但又有细微的不同。大部分的场景都是杂乱无章的，但有时候也会见到我认识的人。\n\n其中一个就是将我逐出的法师。他在一块孤零零的岩石上，被群星环绕。他在那儿用自己的法力封印……某种剧毒的东西，也许就是毒圣。我与骑士领主谈过话，他好像已经找到了关于这个地方的足够的线索。我们只需要你的许可便能动身。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

During mine own exile, I did see things. Like looking through a fractur'd mirror, the same scene did repeat again and again, with tiny variations. Much of what I saw was nonsensical, but some were about people I knew.\n\nOne wast the mage that cast me out. That one wast on a lone rock, surrounded by stars. There he did use his craft to sealeth... something toxic, perhaps the God itself. I did talk with the Lord Commander, and thou seemeth to have enough clues to find this place. We only needeth thy permission to leave.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:417`

## toxoids.7290.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7290.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让骑士准备好！我们立刻出发！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Prepare the knights! We leave at once!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:418`

## toxoids.7295.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7295.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣试炼：封印

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

The Quest for the Toxic God: The Seal

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:419`

## toxoids.7295.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7295.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在$NAME_Sinople$的帮助下取回的数据点让我们可能绘制毒圣的运动轨迹，并发现祂将会去到的下一个星系。然而，一进入到太空，[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]立刻有了反应。\n\n“他就在这。我能感受到他的存在。”我们跟随着[questing_knight_8.GetHisHer]的方向取到了一颗孤零零的卫星。\n\n刚一着陆，我们的骑士便马上遭遇到了此前[questing_knight_6.GetName]和[questing_knight_8.GetName]在过去见过的那位骗术师。\n\n“你们可真是冥顽不灵，”骗术师说：“但这回就是你们最后的任务了。我的主人绝不允许你们愚蠢打得信仰打破虚境的平衡，甚至连他们都未曾如此激怒过虚境。毒圣根本不存在，毒圣骑士团也很快将不复存在！”骗术师话音刚落，我们发现了许多能量信号正在进入这个恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

The data point recovered with $NAME_Sinople$'s help has allowed us to chart the path followed by the Toxic god, and to pinpoint the system where it might have gone next. However, upon entering the space, knight [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName] reacted immediately.\n\n"He's here. I can senseth his presence." We followed [questing_knight_8.GetHisHer] directions to a forlorn moon.\n\nUpon landing, our knights were immediately confronted by none other than the Trickster, previously encountered by [questing_knight_6.GetName], and [questing_knight_8.GetName] in the days of old.\n\n"Thou art persistent," the Trickster said. "But this is the end of thy quest. Mine own master shall not tolerate thy foolish beliefs to fell the balance of the Shroud any more than they already has't. There is no Toxic God, and soon the Toxic Knights will be no more!" As the Trickster fell silent, we registered multiple signatures entering the system.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:420`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7295.a&lang=en)

毒圣试炼：最后的骗术

### English

The Quest for the Toxic God: The Last Trick

## toxoids.7300.desc.sable

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7300.desc.sable&lang=en)

### 中文

在空无一物的空间中浮现出一个虫洞。骑士团的舰队准备好了武器，紧张地等待着。十几艘小型飞船从中出现，这些舰船和黑骑士在几十年前驾驶的颇为相似，他们直接略过我们的舰队笔直飞向那颗卫星。\n\n在地表上，战斗打响了。超自然风暴覆盖着这片大地，骗术师灵能全开，入侵着我们骑士们的心智，制造幻象并使用念动力和各种弹射体阻挡着我们。我们的骑士意志坚定，装甲强韧，但骗术师怪异可怕的灵能却让他们无法靠近一步。\n\n突然，十几个陌生人从风暴中显现，每一个都穿着与黑骑士一样的中子装甲。他们对我们没有展现出敌意，而是径直杀向骗术师。其中一人挥舞着破烂的旗帜，利用内置的扬声器系统，在战场上念出一串的复仇的祷文。\n\n“你这肮脏的骗子！你用毒药杀害我们的穿戴者。我们虽然仅是躯壳，但他们的记忆仍与我们同在。让我来做一个了结吧，清算就在眼前！”\n\n骗术师意识到大事不妙，沮丧地诅咒了起来。这支铠甲之军加入了骑士们的战斗，将骗术师团团围住。[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]从困惑中回过神来，举起了[questing_knight_8.GetHisHer]的武器，将骗术师的头部和躯体切割开来。在这胜利到来的一刻，铠甲军队的行动戛然而止。它们的任务已经完成，运作它们的AI便关机了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Where once there was empty space, a wormhole bloomed. Weapons at the ready, the Order's fleet waited tensely as a dozen of small crafts emerged. The ships were similar to that used by the Sable Knight decades ago. The ships dove straight for the moon, paying no mind to our fleet.\n\nOn the surface, the fight raged on. A supernatural storm covered the field, while the Trickster made full use of their powers, invading the minds of our knights, conjuring illusions and using psychokinesis to hold us back with all manner of projectiles. Our knights were determined and well protected, but the eldritch powers of the Trickster held them at bay.\n\nSuddenly, a dozen of strangers emerged out of the storm, each clad in the same neutronium armor as the Sable Knight. They showed no hostility towards us, and instead charged straight at the Trickster. Brandishing a tattered banner, one of them made use of an inbuilt speaker system to blast a litany of vengeance across the battlefield.\n\n"Thou foul Trickster! With poison didst thou murder our wearers. We are but shells, yet their memory remains with us. Now we finish this. Reckoning is at hand!"\n\nSeeing his end approaching, the Trickster cursed in frustration. The armored host joined with the battling knight to beset the Trickster from all sides. Seizing upon the confusion, [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName] took up [questing_knight_8.GetHisHer] blade, and cleaved the Trickster's head from their body. In this moment of triumph, the armored host fell still. Their purpose fulfilled, the AI that had animated them shut down.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:425`

## toxoids.7300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境实体！骑士团的舰队被骗术师召唤的能量生命体包围了。与此同时，在那个卫星上，我们英勇的骑士遭遇到了狡猾的虚境漫游者召唤出来的虚境兽。我们的骑士被一度击溃，直到骑士领主再度激励起他们：“为了骑士团！”\n\n这声呐喊在战场上回响，骑士们围绕着领主重新列起队形，再次投入战斗。在白刃战的中心，[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]骑士大笑了起来，陶醉于纯粹的战斗中。直至[questing_knight_8.GetHeShe]战死，仍有一件事是没有改变的。\n\n突然，骑士们打开了突破口。面前赫然出现骗术师被孤立的身影。狡猾的虚境漫游者瞄准骑士领主释放了一个灵能冲击波。在千钧一发的时刻，[questing_knight_8.GetName]舍身挡下了这个攻击。而[questing_knight_15.GetName]骑士，就是遭遇过叙阿美勒的那位，则向骗术师送上了[questing_knight_15.GetHisHer]最后的一击。\n\n“异教骑士！人民都要陷入困境，你们所有的知识都会被遗忘！”\n\n骗术师的遗言充满了恶毒的能量。\n\n[questing_knight_8.GetFirstName]躺在地上气若游丝。传记将记录下[questing_knight_8.GetHisHer]的遗嘱：“他杀了我而非我的领主，我的死并没有什么，相比保护我的领主这不值一提。” 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Shroud entities! The Order's fleet was beset by energy beings summoned by the Trickster. At the same time, on the moon itself, our valiant knights were set upon by a host of Shroud beasts conjured by the rogue Shroudwalker. Our knights were driven back until the Lord Commander rallied them: "FOR THE ORDER!"\n\nThe shout echoing across the field of battle, the knights reformed ranks around their Commander and dove once more into the fray. At the heart of the melee, knight [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName] laughed, reveling in the purity of combat. At least one thing remained unchanged while [questing_knight_8.GetHeShe] had been gone.\n\nSuddenly, the knights broke through. Only the Trickster stood before them. The rogue Shroudwalker unleashed a blast of psionic energy aimed at the Lord Commander. In the last moment, [questing_knight_8.GetName] dove to intercept the attack. Knight [questing_knight_15.GetName], famous for [questing_knight_15.GetHisHer] encounter with Syamelle, delivered the final blow to the Trickster.\n\n"Foul knights! May your people fall to woe, and all your knowledge be forgotten!"\n\nThe Trickster's last words sizzled with malign energy.\n\nOn the ground, [questing_knight_8.GetFirstName] was also about to expire. Chronicles would later report [questing_knight_8.GetHisHer] last words: "Rather he slayeth me than my liege, for my death shall not be great harm, not half so much as his." 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:426`

## trickster_curse_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrickster_curse_desc&lang=en)

尽管那位千方百计阻挠我们完成试炼的骗术师现在已经死了，此人却留给了我们一个烂摊子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Although the trickster who so fiercely opposed our quest lies dead, they left us a dour souvenir.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:430`

## toxoids.7305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣试炼：门洞大开

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

The Quest for the Toxic God: Opening the Door

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:431`

## toxoids.7305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在战斗之后，骑士们搜遍了卫星上的每一寸土地。最终，在最南端的地下深处，他们发现了一个骗术师留下的有害的装置。装置出现裂痕，裂痕中能量四溢。随着它的创造者的死去，它也逐渐瓦解；灵能流喷涌而出。很快，这个机械装置便化为了灰烬。\n\n轨道上的舰船们在恒星系的边陲发现了一个怪异的亚空间现象：重力自发地发生变化，显露出了一条此前被阻碍的超空间航道。产生的能量释放了一个强大的电磁脉冲，烧毁了骑士团舰船上的大多数设备。\n\n为了完全了解那天发生的事情，我们必须派出自己的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

In the aftermath of battle, the knights searched every inch of the moon. At last, deep within the southern reaches, they discovered a baneful contraption left by the Trickster. Energy thrummed as cracks appeared in the machine; with the death of its creator, it was gradually unraveling; streams of psionic energy spilled forth. Within moments, the machine was reduced to cinder.\n\nFrom orbit, the ships reported a strange subspace phenomenon at the edge of the system: gravity itself had shifted to accommodate the restoration of a blocked hyperlane. The resulting energies unleashed a powerful EMP, frying most systems aboard the Order's ships.\n\nTo fully understand what transpired that day, we will have to dispatch a fleet of our own.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:432`

## toxoids.7305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7305.a&lang=en)

派遣距离最近的舰队！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Send the nearest fleet!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:433`

## toxoids.7310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣试炼：目睹神界 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

The Quest for the Toxic God: Witnessing Divinity 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:435`

## toxoids.7310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.GetName]是腐蚀气旋的家园，其中潜伏着我们一直在寻找的有毒实体。它具有一个金属喙、几丁质外壳、触手和气体囊。这只太空巨兽发出令人作呕的绿光，看起来不太待见我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

[from.GetName] is home to swirling corrosive mists, and within them lurks the toxic entity that we've been searching for. A metallic maw, a chitinous carapace, tentacles and gas sacks. This spacefaring behemoth glows with a sickly green light. We are not welcome.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:436`

## toxoids.7310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这一切根本无从形容！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

It is too hideous for words!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:437`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7310.b&lang=en)

### English

The god is testing us! Summon the fleet!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:438`

毒性神祗

Toxic Entity

## toxoids.7311.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7311.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.GetName]是腐蚀气旋的家园，其中潜伏着一个有毒实体。它具有一个金属喙、几丁质外壳、触手和气体囊。这只太空巨兽无比壮观，闪耀着令人作呕的绿光，看起来不太待见我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

[from.GetName] is home to swirling corrosive mists, and within them lurks a toxic entity. A metallic maw, a carapace. Tentacles and gas sacks. This spacefaring behemoth is imposing, and glows with a sick green light. We are not welcome.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:440`

那到底是……

### English

What in the...

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:441`

## toxoids.7312.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7312.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣试炼：失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

The Quest for the Toxic God: Failure

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:442`

## toxoids.7312.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7312.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无论[from.GetName]中有什么，它都已经不复存在了。腐蚀气旋是曾经有过这么一个以其为家园的存在，但战斗的遗骸表明它在我们找到它之前就被另一个太空文明摧毁了。尽管很明显在这里存在过的并不是什么神，但所有的迹象都指明了这里。失去了任何进一步的线索，骑士团陷入了绝望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

Whatever was in the [from.GetName], it is now gone. Swirling corrosive mists bear witness to a being that had made it its home, but the remnants of battle suggest it was destroyed by another spacefaring civilization before we tracked it down. Although it is clear that whatever was here was no God, all trails led here. Without any further leads, the Knightly Order is in a state of despair.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:443`

## toxoids.7312.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7312.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不相信这一些……我们绝不。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

We do not believe this... we will not.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:444`

## toxoids.7312.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7312.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

或许某一天，真正的毒圣能被发现，但是我们我们尚且不知该向何处看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

Perhaps someday, the true Toxic God will be found, but we know not where to look.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:445`

## toxoids.7315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣试炼：通过考验

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

The Quest for the Toxic God: Trial Overcome

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:446`

## toxoids.7315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣被打败了，如今听任我们摆布。\n\n分析表明，虽然其分泌物的化学成分与我们在母星上发现的成分相近，但并不完全相同。难道这是真神的后裔？或者还有其他的同类？或者这只是一头普通的太空野兽？无论真相是什么，我们的科学家都认为经过悉心照料它可以恢复健康。我们可以随意差遣它了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

The Toxic God is defeated and lays at our mercy.\n\nAnalysis reveal that while the chemical composition of its secretions is close to what can be found on our homeworld, it is not exactly the same. Is this an offspring of the True God? Could there be others? Or was this merely a beast of the void? Whatever the truth, our scientists speculate it could be nursed back to health. We could have the thing at our beck and call.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:447`

## toxoids.7315.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7315.a&lang=en)

### 中文

噢毒圣啊，将我们的敌人毁灭殆尽！ 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

O Toxic God, smite our enemies! 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:448`

## toxoids.7315.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7315.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

试炼完成。已成功控制§Y毒圣§!，可作为毒化星球的§Y巨像§!。\n允许通过环境改造将§Y剧毒行星改造候选§!转变为宜居星球。\n§R于试炼完毕，许多[knight.GetIcon] 骑士将会逐渐离开骑士团。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

End the quest. Take control of the §YToxic God§!, a §YColossus§! that can toxify worlds.\nEnables terraforming of §YToxic World Terraforming Candidates§! into habitable worlds.\n §RWith the end of the quest, many [knight.GetIcon] Knights will eventually leave the Order.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:449`

## toxoids.7315.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7315.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这不是我们的神。屠戮它！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

This is no god. Slayeth it!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:450`

## toxoids.7315.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7315.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

骗术师终究是对的。这不是我们的神。试炼还要继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

The Trickster was right after all. This is no god. The Quest continues.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:451`

## toxoids.7320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团的命运

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

The Fate of the Order

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:452`

## toxoids.7320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到了毒圣，但正因为这一发现，骑士团失去了目的。渺小的试炼和差事根本不足以匹配骑士展现出来的英勇和荣耀！无精打采、漫无目的，有越来越多的骑士已经离开了骑士团。\n\n今天，骑士领主让所有剩余的骑士做出选择，是留下来，还是在他的祝福下离开。许多人离去，希望能寻找到另一种生活。一部分自发组织起来成为小规模的侠义骑士，寻找新的试炼，在整片星海中遨游。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

The Toxic God has been found, but with this discovery, the Order has lost its purpose. Minor quests and errands are not fit for knights used to valor and glory! Listless and lost, more and more knights have begun to leave the Order.\n\nToday, the Lord Commander gave all remaining knights the choice to stay or leave with his blessing. Many left in search for another life. Some have organized in small groups of errant knights, looking for new quests to pursue across the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:453`

## toxoids.7320.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7320.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一个时代的终结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

The end of an age.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:454`

## toxoids.7320.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7320.a.tt&lang=en)

§R£building£ 骑士团主城建筑现在只能支持更少的[knight.GetIcon]骑士岗位§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

§RThe £building£ Order's Keep Building will now support fewer [knight.GetIcon] Knight Jobs§!.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:455`

## toxoids.7510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

击杀有毒实体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Toxic Entity Slain

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:457`

## toxoids.7510.desc.origin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7510.desc.origin&lang=en)

### 中文

在[toxic_god_system.GetName]恒星系内的实体必然不会是神祇。毕竟，如果是的话，我们又怎么能够杀死它呢？\n\n随着它尸体的最后一次抽搐，它的毒腺猛地爆开，腐蚀性的物质迅速地扩散向整个恒星系。通过紧急超光速操作，我们的舰船成功撤离到安全距离，但这个实体自身则没有那么幸运：它的遗骸在接触到自己毒物的一瞬间立刻分解了，除了它的咽喉什么都没有留下。骑士团把它作为战利品保存了下来，这件战利品仅在特殊场合才会拿出来，作为骑士团成就的终极展示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

The Entity in the [toxic_god_system.GetName] system was not a God. After all, if it was, how could we have slain it?\n\nAs its body convulsed one last time, its venom sack burst open, rapidly distributing corrosive material across the system. Through rapid evasive maneuvers, our fleets managed to reach a safe distance, but the entity itself was not so lucky: struck down at the last by its own poison, its flesh decomposed rapidly, leaving naught but its maw behind. The Knightly Order has taken it into its custody as a trophy to be brought out only on special occasions, as an ultimate demonstration of the grandeur of the Order's achievements.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:458`

## toxoids.7510.desc.not_origin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7510.desc.not_origin&lang=en)

### 中文

我们击杀了在[toxic_god_system.GetName]恒星系中的有毒实体。随着它尸体的最后一次抽搐，它的毒腺猛地爆开，腐蚀性的物质迅速地扩散向整个恒星系。通过紧急超光速操作，我们的舰船成功撤离到安全距离，但这个实体自身则没有那么幸运：它的遗骸在接触到自己毒物的一瞬间立刻分解了，除了它的咽喉什么都没有留下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

We have slain the Toxic Entity in the [toxic_god_system.GetName] system. As its body convulsed one last time, its venom sack burst open, rapidly distributing corrosive material across the system. Through rapid evasive maneuvers, our fleets managed to reach a safe distance, but the entity itself was not so lucky: struck down at the last by its own poison, its flesh decomposed rapidly, leaving naught but its maw behind.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:459`

## toxoids.7510.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7510.A&lang=en)

### 中文

现在看来也并非神圣！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Not so divine, is it now!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:460`

## toxoids.7510.A.reanimators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.7510.A.reanimators&lang=en)

哎呀！就算把尸体都复活了也不够呀

### English

Alas! The remains are not enough to be resurrected

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:461`

## toxoids.8000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

试炼奖励

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

Quest Reward

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:465`

## toxoids.8000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一部分骑士刚刚从一场漫长的试炼中归来。他们的星舰斑驳不已，动力装甲也能看见岁月的蹉跎。这是一场紧张刺激的旅行，所获不多，但都是对于毒圣十分珍贵的讯息。尽管他们经受了艰苦岁月，但是兴致都很高，因为他们没有空手而归，而他们的故事很快会被精心编纂录入编年史中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

A group of knights just came back from a long quest. Their spaceships are battered, and their power armors have seen better days. It was a gruelling journey that yielded scarce yet precious insight about the Toxic God. Despite the hardships endured, spirits are high, for they did not come back empty handed and their stories will make fine additions to the chronicles.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:466`

## toxoids.8005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

要求额外资金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Request for Additional Funds

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:467`

## toxoids.8005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几个世纪以来，国家一直将资源的一大部分倾斜向誓言试炼和骑士团；因此我们最近改变政策，削减骑士预算的做法没有得到好的反响。骑士和扈从们十分不满；大部分人民似乎也持同样的看法，认为我们忘记了自己的职责。骑士领主已经签署了一份正式的要求，责令我们改回原先的财政模式，这个要求得到了帝国内部许多声音的支持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

For centuries, the state has been devoting a large portion of resources to the quest and the Order, and our recent change in policy to reduce the knight's budget has not been well received. The knights and the squires are discontent, and a large portion of the population seems to agree, feeling that we are forgetting our duty. The Lord Commander has issued a formal request to return to the regular funding model, a demand supported by many in our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:468`

## toxoids.8005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们是对的，试炼是我们的主要目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

They're right, the Quest is our main goal.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:469`

## toxoids.8005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不。试炼还得再缓缓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

No. The Quest will have to wait.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:470`

## toxoids.8005.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8005.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以举办一场募捐来安抚他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

We can make a donation to appease them.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:471`

## knight_budget_demand

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aknight_budget_demand&lang=en)

批准额外资金

### English

Granted Additional Funding

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:472`

## toxoids.8010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

百年试炼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

The Hundred Years Quest

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:473`

## toxoids.8010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一百年前，我们手摘星辰。这对于我们、对于誓言试炼来说都是崭新的开始，无穷无尽的机会展现在我们的面前。一个世纪过去，我们的人民感受到了割裂感。尽管我们的成就无从否认，但仍然有声音在质疑骑士和试炼的意义。它们真的只是过时的古董了吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

One hundred years ago, we took to the stars. It was a new beginning for us, and for the Quest, with seemingly infinite opportunities opening to us. A century has passed, and our population feels divided. While our achievements are undeniable, there are voices doubting the purpose of the knights and the Quest. Are they just relics of a bygone age?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:474`

## toxoids.8010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不。我们要举办一场庆典，纪念我们的骑士！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

No. We'll hold a festival to honor our knights!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:475`

## toxoids.8010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没时间理会这样的琐事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

We have no time for such trifles.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:476`

## toxoids.8010.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8010.c&lang=en)

### 中文

是。这是历经百年的一连串失败。必须将此事了结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

Yes. It was a century of failures. Put an end to this.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:477`

## toxoids.8010.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8010.c.tooltip&lang=en)
