§R这会结束试炼，摧毁骑士团，并移除所有相关建筑和区划§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

§RThis will end the quest, destroy the order and remove all associated buildings and districts§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:478`

## toxoids.8015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团主城沦陷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

Fall of the Order's Keep

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:480`

## toxoids.8015.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8015.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

主城沦陷了。许多珍贵的战利品和古董被强盗们洗劫一空。然而，在撤离的一片混乱中，仍然有几名勇敢的骑士成功守住了我们宝贵的研究成果，将文库封印到了一个秘密的房室里面。它们现在应该安全了，但是如果无法直接读取这些只是，我们的骑士团就无法运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

The Keep is lost. Many precious trophies and relics have been taken by looters. However, in the chaos of the evacuation, several brave knights managed to preserve our precious research by sealing the archives within a secret chamber. They should be safe for now, but without direct access to this knowledge, the Order cannot function.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:481`

## toxoids.8015.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8015.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

主城沦陷了。许多珍贵的战利品和古董被强盗们洗劫一空。然而，在撤离的一片混乱中，仍然有几名勇敢的骑士成功守住了我们宝贵的研究成果，将文库封印到了一个秘密的房室里面。它们现在应该安全了，但是如果无法直接读取这些只是，试炼就无法继续进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

The Keep is lost. Many precious trophies and relics have been taken by looters. However, in the chaos of the evacuation, several brave knights managed to preserve our precious research by sealing the archives within a secret chamber. They should be safe for now, but without direct access to this knowledge, the quest cannot continue.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:482`

## toxoids.8015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须不惜一切代价夺回主城！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

We must retake the Keep at all costs!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:483`

## toxoids.8017.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8017.name&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团主城毁灭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

The Destruction of the Order's Keep

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:484`

## toxoids.8017.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8017.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团主城被毁了，仅剩一大堆浮动的残骸。骑士领主已经死亡，一同追随而去的还有绝大多数的骑士和扈从。文库被洗劫一空。损失是不可估量的。仅剩的少许剩余的骑士被召回。骑士团的未来注定走向灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

The Keep has been destroyed, reduced to a mass of floating debris. The Lord Commander is dead, along with most of our knights and squires. The archives have been obliterated. This loss is immeasurable. The handful of surviving knights have been recalled. The future of the Order lies in ruin.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:485`

## toxoids.8017.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8017.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团主城被毁了，仅剩一大堆浮动的残骸。骑士领主已经死亡，一同追随而去的还有绝大多数的骑士和扈从。文库被洗劫一空。损失是不可估量的。在这一切发生之后，我们再也没有办法继续誓言试炼了。仅剩的少许剩余的骑士被召回。骑士团的未来注定走向灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

The Keep has been destroyed, reduced to a mass of floating debris. The Lord Commander is dead, along with most of our knights and squires. The archives have been obliterated. This loss is immeasurable. There is no way that we can continue the quest after this. The handful of surviving knights have been recalled. The future of the Order lies in ruin.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:486`

## toxoids.8017.desc.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8017.desc.c&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团主城隔绝开了，化为了一座浮空的纪念碑。骑士领主已经永远离去，一同追随而去的还有绝大多数的骑士和扈从。与宝库也失去了联系。损失难以估量。硕果仅存的少许骑士被召回。骑士团的未来注定走向灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

The Keep has been sealed off from us, reduced to a floating mausoleum. The Lord Commander is forever lost, along with most of our knights and squires. The archives have been disconnected. This loss is immeasurable. The handful of surviving knights have been recalled. The future of the Order lies in ruin.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:487`

## toxoids.8017.desc.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8017.desc.d&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士团主城隔绝开了，化为了一座浮空的纪念碑。骑士领主已经永远离去，一同追随而去的还有绝大多数的骑士和扈从。与宝库也失去了联系。损失难以估量。从此以往我们再也不可能继续试炼了。硕果仅存的少许骑士被召回。骑士团的未来注定走向灭亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

The Keep has been sealed off from us, reduced to a floating mausoleum. The Lord Commander is forever lost, along with most of our knights and squires. The archives have been disconnected. This loss is immeasurable. There is no way that we can continue the quest after this. The handful of surviving knights have been recalled. The future of the Order lies in ruin.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:488`

## toxoids.8017.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8017.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这不可能……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

It cannot be...

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:489`

## toxoids.8020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毒圣的命运

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

The Fate of the Toxic God

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:490`

## toxoids.8020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在找到了有毒实体后，我们的骑士们失去了毒圣去向的行踪。这两者的关联毋庸置疑，但如果要相信我们的历史档案，那么这个实体与智慧和强大的毒圣原体相比可就相形见绌了。\n\n无论如何，我们寻找毒圣的旅途在今天结束了。我们已经从星系中心的超大质量黑洞中发现了排出的痕量有毒物质。尽管看起来不可思议，但我们确信毒圣就在那儿，但远超我们的能力所及。\n\n骑士领主命令所有骑士全力以赴取得任何与黑洞有关的东西，但成功的希望很小。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

After finding the Toxic Entity, our knights lost the trail of the Toxic God. The connection between the two is unquestionable, but if the archives are to be believed, the entity pales compared to the original, which was immensely more formidable and sentient.\n\nToday, however, our search for the Toxic God finally ends. We have picked up trace amounts of noxious substances expelled from the supermassive black hole at the galactic core. As impossible as it seems, we believe the Toxic God is in there. And very much beyond our reach.\n\nThe Lord Commander has ordered all knights to recover anything even remotely related to the black hole, but there is little hope of success...

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:491`

## toxoids.8025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

收复骑士团主城

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

Reconquering the Order's Keep

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:496`

## toxoids.8025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经收复了骑士团主城。我们的工事和辖地已经被占领者们拆除，化为一片废墟，但它们可以被重建。随着我们重新夺取文库的访问权，我们的骑士誓言试炼将重回正轨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

We have recovered the Order's Keep. Our fortifications and demesne lie in ruins, dismantled by the occupiers, but they can be rebuilt. With access to the archives restored, our knights' quests can resume.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:497`

## toxoids.8025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

光荣的一天！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

A glorious day!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:498`

## toxoids.8030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

凯旋而归

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

A Triumphant Return

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:501`

## toxoids.8030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

降落在我们光复的母星上，被许多人视为我们在追寻毒圣途中的集体胜利。自这片土地初次被这位神明触碰以来，我们历经的重重考验已将自身淬炼成最完美的形态——强大、团结，面对危险绝不退缩。\n\n我们的回归，标志着教团历史翻开了崭新的篇章。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Landing on our restored homeworld is viewed by many as our collective triumph in the quest of the Toxic God. The many trials we have endured since our land was first touched by the deity have forged us into the best version of ourselves. Strong, united, and unflinching in the face of danger.\n\nOur return marks the beginning of a new chapter in the history of the Order.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:502`

## toxoids.8030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.8030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

赞美神！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Praise be!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:503`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apolicy_knightly_duties&lang=en)

## policy_knightly_duties_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apolicy_knightly_duties_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士是我们国家产出的精英。他们忠诚、专注、富有荣誉感，是骑士精神的典范，如果我们准备好让他们从试炼中分心，当我们在星系中大展拳脚时，他们的许多技能将是无价之宝。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

Our Knights are the elite of what our country has to offer. Loyal, devoted, honorable, they are paragons of chivalry, and if we are ready to divert them from the Quest, their many skills could be invaluable as we find our place in the galaxy.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:508`

### 中文

我们的骑士将专注于试炼，心无旁骛。我们将竭尽所能找到毒圣之所在。\n§G试炼会从中获益。§!\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

## holding_knight_commandery_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholding_knight_commandery_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个加固的哨站中，我们的骑士同我们的附庸通力合作，寻找毒圣。我们的附属应当提供给我们资源和数据，以支持教团的工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

This fortified outpost is where our knights collaborate with our vassal in the search for the Toxic God. Our subjects are expected to provide us with resources and data to support the Order's work.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:527`

## job_overlord_knight_commandery_effect_desc

### English

[knight.GetIcon] $job_overlord_knight_plural$ turn $r_alloys$ into $r_stability$ and spawn £defense_army£ §YDefense Armies§! for the Subject, and £unity£ §YUnity§! and £physics_research£ £society_research£ £engineering_research£ §YResearch Points§! for the Overlord.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:528`

## building_knightly_theme_park

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_knightly_theme_park&lang=en)

### 中文

骑士游乐场

### English

Knightly Fair Grounds

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:537`

## building_knightly_theme_park_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_knightly_theme_park_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座建筑乍看上去是一座真正的堡垒，但实际上是一座主题公园，颂扬我们的骑士及其试炼。游客们可以购买自己最喜欢的骑士的亲笔签名册、动作手办以及全息海报。当然，这所设施真正的目的还是为我们的试炼收集情报，同时作为试炼中骑士的一处休整场所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Looking like a true fortress, this building is actually a park glorifying our knights and the Quest. Visitors can buy autographs, action figures, and holoposters of their favorite knights. Of course, the true purpose of the facility is to collect information for our Quest and serve as a resupply point for questing knights.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:538`

## knightly_theme_park_quest_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aknightly_theme_park_quest_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§G每月增加毒圣骑士试炼的进度0.01。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

§GIncreases Toxic Knights Quest progress by 0.01 per month.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:539`

## low_motivation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alow_motivation&lang=en)

### 中文

低动力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Low Motivation

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:543`

## low_motivation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alow_motivation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在意识潜入虚境后，大众感受到愉悦感被抽离了：以前的愉悦感觉变得迟钝，即使是最简单的任务也很难激发动力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

Following a mind-delve into the Shroud, the populace feels drained of joy: formerly pleasant sensations are dulled, and it has become hard to dredge up motivation for even the simplest of tasks.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:544`

尽管誓言试炼十分艰辛，但我们的骑士充满了活力，跃跃欲试。

## demotivated_knights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ademotivated_knights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

试炼太漫长，目标太遥远。有时候，它看上去有一点……漫无目的。骑士的士气有点轻微的受挫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

## despair_of_the_knightly_order_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adespair_of_the_knightly_order_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多年以来一直搜寻毒圣的任务最后竟以失败告终。由于没有继续探索的目标，许多人开始质疑继续进行试炼的意义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

The long search for the Toxic God has ended in failure. With no further leads to explore, many question the point of continuing the quest.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:552`

### English

Overtuned

## origin_overtuned_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_overtuned_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个文明不择手段地增强自己的有机体，无论风险，无论成本。毕竟，与其在沉默中消亡，不如光鲜亮丽地燃烧殆尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

This civilization uses any and all means of organic enhancement, no matter the risk, no matter the cost. After all, it is better to burn out brightly than fade away into obscurity.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:557`

## origin_overtuned_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_overtuned_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

开局时可以选择高级生物学特质，比一般的特质更强，成本更低廉，同时却带有极高的风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Starts with advanced biological traits available, which are stronger and cheaper than normal traits but carry extraordinary risks.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:558`

## origin_tooltip_overtuned_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_overtuned_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—$SPECIES_TRAITS$可以选择['concept_overtuned_traits', $origin_overtuned$]特质。$NEW_LINE$—强夺天工特质：$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—可由['concept_damn_the_consequences']法令进一步增强$TABBED_NEW_LINE$—可以通过§I基因修饰§!进行添加或移除\n—$ADDITIONAL_TECHNOLOGIES_STRING$['concept_gene_tailoring', £society£$tech_gene_tailoring$]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

- $SPECIES_TRAITS$ Access to ['concept_overtuned_traits', $origin_overtuned$] Traits$NEW_LINE$- Overtuned Traits:$TABBED_NEW_LINE$- Can be boosted by the ['concept_damn_the_consequences'] Edict$TABBED_NEW_LINE$- Can be added and removed through §IGenetic Modification§!\n- $ADDITIONAL_TECHNOLOGIES_STRING$ ['concept_gene_tailoring', £society£ $tech_gene_tailoring$]

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:559`

## START_SCREEN_OVERTUNED

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_OVERTUNED&lang=en)

### 中文

自然是个不温不火的发明家，而进化则如同冰川流动一般迟缓。为了发展繁荣我们需要身强力健、敏捷如飞并且脑力超群，而且我们等不了一百万年，只争朝夕。自然选择吝啬于赐予我们礼物，我们只能自己动手。\n\n于是我们的文明的原则便如此建立起来了：我们要无限制地强化我们的身躯。这些强化把我们的星球统一了起来，并且将带领我们走向更遥远的未来。\n\n基因工程赐予我们超强大脑、无尽膂力和无上勇气，我们定能在群星间繁荣昌盛……这也是设计完美的生物才能拥有的特权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Nature is a tepid inventor. Evolution is a glacial process. To prosper we require strength, speed and brain power - now, not in a million years. Natural selection is miserly with its gifts and so we must improve ourselves.\n\nThus our civilization is built on the principle of unrestrained enhancement of our physical bodies. These enhancements have allowed us to unite our world under one banner, and now they will lead us even further.\n\nWith genetically crafted brains, brawn, and bravery, we shall prosper among the stars... As only a perfectly designed species can.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:560`

## START_SCREEN_OVERTUNED_NOMADS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_OVERTUNED_NOMADS&lang=en)

### 中文

自然是个不温不火的发明家，而进化则如同冰川流动一般迟缓。为了发展繁荣，我们需要身强力健、敏捷如飞并且脑力超群——而且我们等不了一百万年，只争朝夕。自然选择吝啬于赐予我们礼物，我们只能自己动手。\n\n虚空不会给弱者划出保留地。这些强化已让我们足以应对它的苛刻要求，并将我们的人民团结在同一面旗帜之下，带领我们走向更遥远的未来。\n\n基因工程赐予我们超强大脑、无尽膂力和无上勇气，我们定能在群星间繁荣昌盛……这也是设计完美的生物才能拥有的特权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

Nature is a tepid inventor. Evolution is a glacial process. To prosper we require strength, speed and brain power - now, not in a million years. Natural selection is miserly with its gifts and so we must improve ourselves.\n\nThe void makes no allowances for weakness. These enhancements have hardened us against its demands and united our people under one banner. Now they will lead us even further.\n\nWith genetically crafted brains, brawn, and bravery, we shall prosper among the stars... As only a perfectly designed species can.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:561`

## civic_toxic_baths_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_toxic_baths_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个文明会举办突变沐浴仪式，在职业护理员的监管下使用高危药品。“灌注生命力”的过程也许会让不谙内情的旁观者胆战心惊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

This civilization engages in ritualistic mutagenic bathing, with professional attendants overseeing the use of highly dangerous substances. This 'invigorating' process may appall uninitiated onlookers.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:582`

## civic_corporate_toxic_baths_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_corporate_toxic_baths_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个文明会进行突变沐浴，在职业护理员的监管下使用高危药品。“灌注生命力”的过程被营销成了促进个人发展与福利的必要步骤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

This civilization engages in mutagenic bathing, with professional attendants overseeing the use of highly dangerous substances. This 'invigorating' process is marketed as a necessary step in wellbeing and personal development.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:583`

## civic_hive_toxic_baths_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hive_toxic_baths_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个蜂巢思维会使用诱变池，由特化护理蜂监管新生工蜂成长。该过程会严重侵扰当地环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

This hive mind utilizes mutagenic pools, with specialized attendants overseeing the care of all drones. This process is highly intrusive for the local environment.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:584`

## civic_machine_toxic_baths_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_machine_toxic_baths_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个机械帝国改良了基础无人机外壳的生产过程，在精心编程的终端控制下高强度敷用强效润滑剂。这一“调优”过程靠的是向来作为废物的工业副产品，如今则被用来达到最高的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

This machine empire improved upon its basic drone-shell production through the vigorous application of highly aggressive lubrication agents, controlled by carefully programmed terminals. This 'optimized' process relies on typically wasted byproducts from the industries that are now repurposed for maximal efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:585`

## councilor_toxic_baths_tt

## building_toxic_bath_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_toxic_bath_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管诱变水疗中心是极度先进的理疗机构，文献记载其对健康有诸多益处，但是对某些人来说，这种方法还是略显恶心。浴池中的每一滴液体，相较于星系中的其他违禁品来说，都更加具有诱变性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

Though Mutagenic Spas are highly advanced therapeutic centers with well-documented health benefits, to some, the methods employed remain disgusting. Each drop of bath liquid is more mutagenic than most banned substances in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:598`

## building_toxic_bath_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_toxic_bath_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

诱变重构池完美展现了蜂巢思维在拓展其无限意识过程中所达到的至高境界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Mutagenic Permutation Pools are the perfect expression of the heights that a hive mind can achieve when utterly focused on the goal of maintaining the ever-expanding consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:599`

## building_toxic_bath_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_toxic_bath_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超润滑洗槽，是针对复杂单元维护中常见的摩擦、过热及静电等问题所提出的极具逻辑性的解决方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Hyper Lubrication Basins are the most logical answer to emerging problems of friction, over-heating, and static electricity during the maintenance process of complex units. 

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:600`

## job_bath_attendant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_bath_attendant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在诱变水疗中心受过训练的护理师。他们毕生的目标就是确保他们的客户通过各种各样的服务延续健康，包括纵情解毒和对基因衰老进行小心修复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

Trained Attendants for the Mutagenic Spas. Their purpose in life is to ensure their clients' continued health through a variety of services, including intemperate detoxification and carefully curated genotype degeneration.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:608`

### 中文

尽管诱变水疗中心是极度先进的理疗机构，并配备有记载详尽的健康增益，但是对某些人来说，这种方法还是略显恶心。浴池中的每一滴液体，相较于星系中的其他违禁品来说，都更加具有诱变性。

## civic_relentless_industrialists_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_relentless_industrialists_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生命，便是开发。这个文明建立在“最彻底地活用生命”这一原则之上，致力于从能够用于开发的每一克物质、每一片瓣膜上榨取最大的利益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

To live is to exploit. This civilization is built around the principle of living life to the fullest, in the sense of squeezing the maximum profit from every last ounce of matter and membrane available for exploitation.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:659`

## building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有一些人视该设施里面所执行的事务为不安全的、危险的、短视的，但没人可以否认它带来的生产力的提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

Although some might deem the practices promoted in this facility to be unsafe or dangerously short-sighted, one cannot argue with the productivity numbers they produce.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:667`

## building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_coordinated_fulfillment_center_2&lang=en)

### English

Universal Productivity Alignment Facility

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:668`

## building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_coordinated_fulfillment_center_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过无休止的数学运算和习以为常的性能评估，工业生产力能够被推到一个前所未有的高度。即便这么做足以让人无暇顾及其他，比如人口繁衍和环境方面的问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:600`

### English

Through tireless number-crunching and ubiquitous performance reviews, industrial productivity can be pushed to hitherto unmatched levels. Even if this leaves little time for distractions such as reproduction and environmental concerns.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:669`

## toxoids.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[Root.GetName]的竭泽而渔已经对环境造成了显著，乃至不可逆转的变化。工业污染引发的失控温室效应造成了严重的气候破坏。放射性物质的泄露更是导致大片[Root.GetPlanetMoon]区域不再宜居。\n\n这种变化已经来到了关键的十字路口，除非我们改变开发手段，减少对星球环境的影响，不然的话就会遭受灭顶之灾，留下的将是一个不宜居且充满辐射的星球，星球上也会只剩波涛汹涌的海洋与死寂的不毛之地，如同预言末日的人们所给过的最恐怖的警告一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

Our exploitation of [Root.GetName] has caused significant - perhaps irreversible - changes to the world's environment. Industrial pollution has caused significant climatic disruption via a runaway greenhouse effect, and rampant radioactive leaks have rendered large swaths of the [Root.GetPlanetMoon] uninhabitable by conventional means.\n\nThe process has now reached a tipping point. Unless we change our ways and start mitigating our impact on the planetary environment, we will be unable to prevent outright calamity: an inhospitable and irradiated world to match the worst of doomsayers' warnings, characterized by turbulent seas and unbroken lifeless wastelands.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:677`

### English

## relentless_industrialists_situation_type

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelentless_industrialists_situation_type&lang=en)

### English

## relentless_industrialists_situation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelentless_industrialists_situation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们不采取措施，我们对[Target.GetName]的竭泽而渔会让当地成为一个死寂星球。我们必须决定对这个议题抱持什么态度：我们是要改变做法，还是说当下的情况是可接受的——甚至是期待中的——工业化进程中的代价？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:609`

### English

If we take no action, our exploitation of [Target.GetName] will rapidly render it a Tomb World. We must decide where we stand on this issue: will we have to change our ways, or is this an acceptable - or even desirable - price of industrial progress?

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:681`

## toxoids.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在对于[Target.GetName]环境的破坏已经达到了不可逆转的程度。我们已经无力阻止放射性废料遍布[Target.GetPlanetMoon]地表了。不幸的是，民众对此尤为不满，他们组织集会抗议我们的统治，并宣称是我们对于利益的过度追求“毁掉了一切”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

The environmental damage to [Target.GetName] is now irreversible. Nothing we do can prevent the spread of radioactive waste across the [Target.GetPlanetMoon]'s surface. Unfortunately, the populace has not greeted this news with pleasure, organizing rallies against our leadership and complaining that our relentless pursuit of profit has "ruined everything".

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:698`

## toxoids.10.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.10.A&lang=en)

### 中文

他们无视了我们的优先战略目标，太遗憾了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

It is sad that they are blind to our priorities.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:699`

## toxoids.10.A.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.10.A.tooltip&lang=en)

在我们的行星沦为死寂星球时，我们会遭受低稳定度的惩罚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

We will suffer low stability while our planets are turned into Tomb Worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:700`

## toxoids.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在对于[Target.GetName]环境的破坏已经达到了不可逆转的程度。我们已经无力阻止放射性废料遍布[Target.GetPlanetMoon]地表了。民众们对此无可奈何，因为需要努力实现下个月的生产目标，他们必须更加拼命地工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

The environmental damage to [Target.GetName] is now irreversible. Nothing we do can prevent the spread of radioactive waste across the [Target.GetPlanetMoon]'s surface. The response from the populace has been a great collective shrug as they bury themselves even deeper in their work, trying desperately to hit next month's productivity targets...

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:703`

## toxoids.15.A

他们本该如此。

### English

As well they should.

## toxoids.25.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.25.name&lang=en)

### 中文

以进步之名

### English

For the Sake of Progress

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:706`

## toxoids.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造已经完成：[Target.GetName]现在变成了一个贫瘠的、遍布辐射的废土。另一方面，更重要的是，我们的工业器械完全没有受到损伤，因此我们仍然可以达成自己的生产指标。没有什么比指标更重要的了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

The transformation is complete: [Target.GetName] is now a barren, irradiated wasteland. More importantly, however, our industrial apparatus remains intact, so we are set to hit our production targets. Besides that, nothing else matters.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:707`

## toxoids.25.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.25.A&lang=en)

值得付出的代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

A price worth paying.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:708`

### English

Our §Y$building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_plural$§! will now provide even greater industrial output bonuses.

## toxoids.30.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

改造的知识

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

Transformative Knowledge

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:711`

## toxoids.30.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.30.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造已经完成：[Target.GetName]现在变成了一个贫瘠的、遍布辐射的废土。我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]已经透彻地研究了这一过程，并保证将该过程复制到其他星球上是完全安全的，但是仍然有部分人对我们为什么需要这项技术能力表示了忧虑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

The transformation is complete: [Target.GetName] is now a barren, irradiated wasteland. Our [Owner.GetResearchers] have studied the process and promise they will be able to replicate it safely on other worlds, though some expressed unease as to exactly why we needed this ability.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:712`

## toxoids.30.desc.thisisfine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.30.desc.thisisfine&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造已经完成：[Target.GetName]现在变成了一个贫瘠的、遍布辐射的废土。我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]已经透彻地研究了这一过程，并保证只要有相关需求，我们就可以安全地将该过程复制到其他星球上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

The transformation is complete: [Target.GetName] is now a barren, irradiated wasteland. Our [Owner.GetResearchers] have studied the process and promise they will be able to replicate it safely on other worlds, should we see the need.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:713`

### English

Knowledge is power.

我们现在可以通过环境改造将行星转变为§Y死寂星球§!。

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:717`

## toxoids.35.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.35.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们孜孜不倦的清理工作将[Target.GetName]带离了毁灭灾厄的边缘。这个星球的气候稳定了下来，我们也从这个过程中学习到了足够的知识，确保类似的情况不会在未来复演，同时不会挫伤我们雄心勃勃的生产指标——尽管我们需要调配一些资源用于纾污花费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

Our extensive cleanup efforts have brought [Target.GetName] back from the brink of disaster. The world's climate has now been stabilized, and we have learnt enough from the process to prevent this from happening in future without lowering our ambitious production targets - though we will have to set aside some resources as mitigation costs...

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:718`

## toxoids.35.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.35.A&lang=en)

### 中文

结果好就是真的好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

All's well that ends well.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:719`

## TOMB_WORLD_FROM_INDUSTRY_MESSAGE_TYPE_DESC

### 中文

我们对工业化的贪念带来的是经年累月的环境破坏，现在§Y[This.GetName]§!已经成为了一个§Y死寂星球§!。

## relentless_industrialists_tomb_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelentless_industrialists_tomb_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

过度开发已经让这个行星成为了一处漫布辐射的荒芜废土。人们因为一连串事件的发生变得尤其沮丧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

Excessive exploitation has turned this planet into a radioactive wasteland. The populace is dismayed at this turn of events.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:725`

## policy_relentless_industrialists_policy_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apolicy_relentless_industrialists_policy_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生存就是不择手段。但是真的要将其投入使用一定会产生某种后果。我们可以选择将精力花费实现更多目标，或者设法减轻其最坏的影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:651`

### English

To live is to exploit. But to exploit to the degree that we practice it has consequences. We can choose to channel our energies towards the attainment of more, or seek to mitigate its worst effects.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:731`

## relentless_industrialists_study_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelentless_industrialists_study_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将一个星球辐射化，这个过程中有太多我们可以学习的知识。\n\n允许我们将行星环境改造成为§Y死寂星球§!。\n减少§Y$building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_plural$§!造成的宜居性惩罚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

There is much to learn from the process of irradiating a planet.\n\nAllows us to terraform planets into §YTomb Worlds§!.\nReduced habitability penalties from §Y$building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_plural$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:737`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelentless_industrialists_cleanup_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应当采取措施，缓解我们在大自然身体上留下的伤痕。\n\n拥有§Y$building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_plural$§!的行星不再随时间推移会被转化为§Y死寂星球§!，但是该建筑现在会要消耗£unity£ §Y凝聚力§!维护费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

We shall take steps to mitigate our environmental footprint.\n\nPlanets with §Y$building_coordinated_fulfillment_center_1_plural$§! will no longer turn into §YTomb Worlds§! over time, but the building will now cost a £unity£ §YUnity§! upkeep.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:740`

## RELENTLESS_INDUSTRIALISTS_STUDY_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARELENTLESS_INDUSTRIALISTS_STUDY_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

安全辐射化的秘密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

The Secret to Safer Radiation

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:742`

## RELENTLESS_INDUSTRIALISTS_STUDY_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARELENTLESS_INDUSTRIALISTS_STUDY_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应当学习如何安全地将一个行星辐射化，不给当地人群造成过大的不便。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

We shall study how to safely irradiate a planet, without unduly inconveniencing the local population.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:743`

## toxoids.45.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.45.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[This.GetResearchers]正在研究我们的工业将环境转为辐射化的整个过程，并承诺未来他们能够安全地将葱郁的生态系统转变为辐射环境，同时不会对当地人群造成过量的伤害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

Our [This.GetResearchers] have studied the processes of irradiation our industries employ, and promise they will now be able to safely convert vibrant ecosystems into radioactive environments without causing excessive harm to the local population.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:745`

## civic_scavengers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_scavengers&lang=en)

### 中文

拾荒者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

Scavengers

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:748`

## civic_scavengers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_scavengers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个社会，即使是处于最残酷的战争年代，也不会有东西被浪费掉：敌人的一切科技都会被解密，因为拾荒者会随着作战舰队一起行动，回收利用任何有打捞价值的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

In this society, nothing goes to waste, even in the grimmest times of war: no enemy technology will go undiscovered, as teams of scrappers accompanying the war fleets scrape wreckages of anything and everything worth salvaging.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:749`

## civic_scavengers_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_scavengers_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—研究§H残骸§!不仅可以获得$r_research$，还可以从损毁的舰船上回收一部分§Y建造花费§!。\n—我们可以打捞一些§H残骸§!并将其修复为§H舰船§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

- Researching §HDebris§! grants both $r_research$ and a portion of other §YResource Costs§! of the destroyed fleet\n- §HShips§! can be salvaged from some §HDebris§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:750`

### English

$civic_scavengers_effects$\n- The £building£ §Y$building_industrial_subsidiary$§! $megacorp_civic_branch_office_building_effect$

## incubator_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aincubator_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

提供§G+30%§!至§R-10%§!的人口增长速度，反比于行星上人口的数量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

Grants between §G+30%§! and §R-10%§! Pop Growth Speed, inversely proportional to the number of pops on the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:756`

## trait_incubator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_incubator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L当被隔离开或者种群的人口数量很少时，拥有这个特质的物种就会集中精力以一种其他物种难以接受的程度繁衍生产。反之，在人口众多时，该效应则会反转，繁衍会更加困难。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:673`

### English

§LWhen isolated or few in numbers, a species with this trait naturally adapts and is able to focus on procreation with an intentness others would consider extreme. However, when in large numbers, this effect is reversed, and they find reproduction more difficult.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:757`

### 中文

非毒性人口的幸福度：§R$@noxious_happiness_penalty|%$§!\n    —每个非毒性人口导致幸福度（不包含不受幸福度影响的人口）：§G$@noxious_happiness_bonus|%$§!（最大：§G+40%§!）

## trait_noxious_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_noxious_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L在拥有这种特质的生物附近会感受到极端的不悦，由于对其他人来说实在难以忍受，它们也经常要承受各种非难。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

§LExtremely unpleasant to be in the vicinity of, beings with this trait take vindication from being insufferable to everyone else.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:765`

## ap_detox_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aap_detox_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在环境改造方面的最新技术突破能够让我们在一类新的行星上面孕育生命——我们此前认为这类行星极度致命，根本无法改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

Our newest advancements in terraforming allow us to breathe new life into a class of planets formerly considered irrevocably deadly and beyond salvation.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:774`

## detox_tox_worlds

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adetox_tox_worlds&lang=en)

### 中文

能够将§Y剧毒行星改造候选§!通过环境改造转变为宜居的星球。\n在我们的国境内有§Y[This.num_toxic_terraform_candidates]§!颗这样的行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Enables terraforming of §YToxic World Terraforming Candidates§! into habitable worlds.\nWe have §Y[This.num_toxic_terraform_candidates]§! of these within our border.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:775`

## MESSAGE_TOXIC_TERRAFORM_FOUND_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMESSAGE_TOXIC_TERRAFORM_FOUND_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

剧毒星球[From.GetName]令人意外地可以成为潜在的环境改造候选星球。如果能将其彻底清理干净，它在未来能为我们的新家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

The toxic world of [From.GetName] has surprisingly turned out to be a potential Terraforming Candidate. If it were to be cleansed it could be a future home for us.

### 中文

发现剧毒环境改造候选

## toxoids.500.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.500.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[From.GetName]的详细调查得出了结论，尽管由于其大气层内充满剧毒气体，现阶段生命不适合栖居，但是它具有维持可支持生命的大气层的潜质。通过合作筹划，我们或许能够将[From.GetPlanetMoon]的有毒物质降解成为毒性更加轻微的化合物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

Our detailed survey of [From.GetName] has revealed that although it is currently unsuitable for life due to the noxious gases present in its atmosphere, it is capable of sustaining a hospitable atmosphere. Through a concerted effort, we may be able to break the [From.GetPlanetMoon]'s toxins down into less lethal compounds.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:781`

## toxoids.500.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.500.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

$toxoids.500.desc.intro$\n\n对这个[From.GetPlanetMoon]进行环境改造理论上是可能的，但是我们目前还没有积累足够的技术手段，在合理的时间框架内完成这项壮举。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

$toxoids.500.desc.intro$\n\nTerraforming this [From.GetPlanetMoon] would theoretically be possible, but we do not yet possess the means to accomplish this monumental task within a realistic timeframe.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:782`

## toxoids.500.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxoids.500.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

$toxoids.500.desc.intro$\n\n巨大的能耗令人望而却步，但我们确实已经掌握了逐步将这颗[From.GetPlanetMoon]环境改造为可以再度支持生命的星球的技术手段了。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

$toxoids.500.desc.intro$\n\nThe energy costs would be daunting, but we do have the means to gradually terraform this [From.GetPlanetMoon] into something that would support life once again.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:783`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_rotten_soil&lang=en)

### 中文

£blocker£腐烂温床

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

£blocker£ Rotten Soil

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:791`

## d_tainted_snowcaps_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_tainted_snowcaps_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经连绵不绝的毒山，一度占据了整个星球，现在只剩最后一小片毒物的残留。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

The last toxic remains of the once enormous mountains of poison that dominated this world.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:792`

## d_poisonous_algae_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_poisonous_algae_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们对这个星球执行第一轮清理后，这些有毒海藻仍然幸存了下来。尽管它对于殖民活动的侵害还不足以造成阻碍，但还是具有相当强的适应能力……有时候会变得很恼人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

This noxious algae survived the initial cleansing of this world. While it is not aggressive enough to prevent colonization, it has proven highly adaptable... and quite simply a nuisance.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:793`

## d_venomous_insects_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_venomous_insects_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

恶毒害虫的最后一窝，我们本希望在环境改造工作前期就把它们一股脑消灭掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

The lost broods of venomous vermin that we had hoped to extinguish during the early days of our terraforming effort.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:794`

## d_unpleasant_atmosphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_unpleasant_atmosphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

空气中恶心的气味和味道，是这个行星剧毒过往的残留印记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

The disgusting taste and smell of the air are what remains of this planet's toxic past.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:795`

## d_rotten_soil_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_rotten_soil_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对其进行清洗和翻新之后，即使是这样的腐泥也能对我们大有裨益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Even this rotten soil will be useful to us, once it has been refreshed and reinvigorated.

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:796`

## trait_spliced_adaptability

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_spliced_adaptability&lang=en)

### 中文

移接适应力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Spliced Adaptability

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:800`

## trait_spliced_adaptability_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_spliced_adaptability_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L对基因片段的各处进行裁切和移接使他们可以在几乎任何环境下生存。这个过程的副作用会造成一定数量的死亡，但过程总体可控。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

§LA few genetic snips and slices here and there and they can survive nearly anywhere. The process comes with an acceptable number of deaths from side effects.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:801`

## spliced_adaptability_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspliced_adaptability_tooltip&lang=en)

宜居性：§G+20%§!

### English

Habitability: §G+20%§!

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_juiced_power&lang=en)

## trait_juiced_power_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_juiced_power_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L根据需要释放的肾上腺素和超强力生长激素使其变得无比强健。只需要无视体内偶尔发出撕裂的声音即可。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:712`

### English

§LAdrenaline released on demand and hyper-potent growth hormones have led to a massive increase in strength. Just ignore the occasional internal tearing.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:804`

## trait_crack_miner_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_crack_miner_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L超强的手眼协调，几乎无限的耐力和自净化的肺部催生出了完美的采矿团队。只可惜他们因远离阳光而变得易于骨折。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

§LSupreme hand-eye coordination, near endless endurance and self-purifying lungs have created entire crack teams of miners. Sadly they get brittle without sunlight.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:807`

## trait_farm_hands_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_farm_hands_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L附肢越多，种地就越强。其中一些附肢甚至可以在主体进入睡眠后继续工作。至于主体被自己新的附肢勒死一事，我们最新的调查显示纯属谣言。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

§LThe more appendages, the higher the farming output. Some appendages can even operate while their host is asleep. Rumors of hosts strangled by their new limbs have been deemed unfounded in our latest investigation.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:810`

## trait_technical_skill_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_technical_skill_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L额外的内部器官可以大大提高对电和磁场的先天敏感性。受改造者在反应堆里听到尖叫和低语的报告应该被忽略。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

§LExtra internal organs grant a vastly increased innate sensitivity to electricity and magnetic fields. Reports of recipients hearing screams and voices from the reactors should be ignored.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:813`

## trait_commercial_genius_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_commercial_genius_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L通过有意识地调整适应能力并适当降低同理心，可以激发出卓越的交易天赋。在追求卓越的商业主导地位时，这一目标的重要性远远超越了可能产生的任何副作用。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

§LBy tweaking agreeability and dialing down empathy, it is possible to artificially kindle a spark of trading genius. The pursuit of unmatched commercial dominance outweighs any potential side effects.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:816`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_crafted_smiles_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L面部特征的轻微调整、肌肉发育增强和松弛相互结合，再加上一些外在化学物质，创造了永恒完美的微笑。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

§LSlightly shifted facial features, a mix of enhanced and slackened muscular development - and of course outside chemicals - have created the perfect smile. Forever.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:819`

## trait_low_maintenance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_low_maintenance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L降低消耗、排泄和休息率使其事半功倍。然而对高蛋白食物的需求提升了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

§LReduced consumption, excretion, and rest rates have resulted in twice the beings for half the price. However, demand for protein-rich meals has increased.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:822`

增强智能

## trait_artificial_intelligence_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_artificial_intelligence_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L脑子越多智力越高。在受改造者体内的关键位置增加额外的思考器官可以最大化他们的认知能力。尽管各个脑子间并不总能达成一致。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

§LMore brains equals more brain power. Extra thinking organs have been added in strategic areas of the recipient's body to ensure maximum cognitive capacity. Even if they don't always agree.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:825`

## trait_gene_mentorship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_gene_mentorship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L通过基因层面重构的“本能知识”和外部化学刺激的组合，我们得以大大加速学习过程。尽管这会导致极为罕见的妄想症个案出现。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

§LBy combining genetically redesigned 'instinctive knowledge' and outside chemical stimuli, we have succeeded in vastly accelerating our learning capacities. Although it has resulted in some extremely paranoid individuals.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:828`

### 中文

§L按需狂热：信念只不过是激素的调和，我们已对其了如指掌。至于信念最终将走向何处取决于后续如何引导。§!

### 中文

§L大脑作为计算设备其实并非在100%功率下运转。然而我们已经大大超出了平均水平。如果遇到问题请重新启动。§!

## trait_preplanned_growth_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_preplanned_growth_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§L计划繁育、计划吸引、计划婚配。按需增长可以塑造强大而稳定的人口——只要前前后后进行一些简单的基因编辑即可得偿所愿。产生嫉妒的个体会被引导前往最近的生育中心，进行强制训练。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

§LScheduled fertility, scheduled interest, scheduled compatibility. Population growth on demand for a strong and stable population - with a few minor edits here and there. Jealous individuals are to be directed to the nearest Fertility Center for mandatory training.§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:837`

## edict_damn_the_consequences_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aedict_damn_the_consequences_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该法令将我们的基因修饰推向极致，使其效果翻倍，但同时也使出现恶性副作用的概率有所上升，包括突发灾难性的爆燃。\n\n§R无惧后果将无视['concept_edict_fund']，并始终消耗你每月$unity_with_icon$收入的80%！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/toxoids_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

This Edict pushes our genetic modifications to the very limit, doubling their effect but also causing an increased amount of terrible side-effects, including sudden catastrophic combustion.\n\n§RDamn the Consequences ignores ['concept_edict_fund'] and will always use 80% of your monthly $unity_with_icon$ income!§!

Source: `localisation/english/toxoids_l_english.yml:844`
